# Architecture

System design reference for the Dr. Bartender platform.

## High-Level Architecture

```
┌─────────────────────┐        HTTPS         ┌─────────────────────┐
│   React Frontend    │ ───────────────────── │   Express Backend   │
│   (Vercel)          │   /api/* requests     │   (Render)          │
│                     │ ◄──────────────────── │                     │
│   Port 3000 (dev)   │     JSON responses    │   Port 5000         │
└─────────────────────┘                       └────────┬────────────┘
                                                       │
                                              ┌────────▼────────────┐
                                              │   PostgreSQL        │
                                              │   (Neon)            │
                                              └─────────────────────┘
```

**Dev mode**: React dev server proxies `/api` to `localhost:5000` via the `proxy` field in `client/package.json`.

**Production**: Frontend is a static SPA on Vercel. It calls the backend at `REACT_APP_API_URL` (set at build time). CORS on the backend allows `CLIENT_URL`.

## Authentication Flow

```
1. User registers or logs in
   POST /api/auth/register  or  POST /api/auth/login
   → Server hashes password (bcryptjs), creates/verifies user
   → Returns JWT (7-day expiry, payload: { userId })

2. Client stores token in localStorage

3. Every API request includes:
   Authorization: Bearer <token>
   (set automatically by axios interceptor in client/src/utils/api.js)

4. Server middleware (server/middleware/auth.js):
   → Verifies JWT signature with JWT_SECRET
   → Fetches user from DB (id, email, role, onboarding_status, can_hire, can_staff)
   → Attaches to req.user
   → Blocks deactivated/rejected staff with 403

5. Role-based access:
   - staff: onboarding pages, portal, application
   - admin: full admin dashboard access
   - manager: admin dashboard access (same as admin in routing)

6. On 401 response: client clears token, redirects to /login
```

**Public routes** (no auth): Drink plans and proposals use UUID tokens in the URL (`/plan/:token`, `/proposal/:token`) instead of authentication.

## Error Handling

The app uses a layered error display system across all surfaces.

| Surface | Component | When to use |
|---|---|---|
| Field-level inline | `<FieldError>` | Server attributes failure to a specific field (`fieldErrors: { email: '...' }`) |
| Form-level banner | `<FormBanner>` | Operation failure not tied to one field; placed immediately above the submit button, auto-scrolls into view |
| Toast | `useToast()` | System events not tied to a form: success confirmations, session expiry, network drops |
| Modal fallback | `<ErrorBoundary>` | Unhandled React error — page can't render |

### Server error envelope

All error responses (4xx and 5xx) use this shape:

```json
{
  "error": "Human-readable message",
  "code": "OPTIONAL_MACHINE_CODE",
  "fieldErrors": { "fieldName": "field-specific message" }
}
```

`error` is required and backward compatible with the original `{ error: "..." }` shape. `code` is optional, machine-readable, used for Sentry tagging and frontend special handling. `fieldErrors` is optional, drives field-level inline display.

### AppError class hierarchy (`server/utils/errors.js`)

| Class | Status | Code | Use |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ValidationError(fieldErrors, message?)` | 400 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | Field-attributable input failure |
| `ConflictError(message, code?)` | 409 | `CONFLICT` (or custom) | State conflict (duplicate, locked, already-paid) |
| `NotFoundError(message?)` | 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Resource doesn't exist |
| `PermissionError(message?)` | 403 | `PERMISSION_DENIED` | Authenticated but not allowed |
| `ExternalServiceError(service, originalError, message?)` | 502 | `EXTERNAL_SERVICE_ERROR` | Stripe/R2/Twilio/Resend/Nominatim failure |
| `PaymentError(message, code?)` | 402 | `PAYMENT_FAILED` (or custom) | Stripe card decline, insufficient funds, post-payment mutation conflicts |

Routes throw via `asyncHandler`-wrapped handlers; the global error middleware in `server/index.js` formats the response envelope and reports unknown errors to Sentry.

### Observability

- **Server:** `@sentry/node` initialized at the top of `server/index.js`. Gated on `SENTRY_DSN_SERVER` env var (silent in dev). Redaction lives in `server/utils/sentryScrub.js`, NOT inline in `index.js`, so it can be unit-tested (index.js starts the app on require). It exports two hooks because Sentry has two pipelines that do not share one: `scrubErrorEvent` (`beforeSend`, ERROR events) and `scrubTransactionEvent` (`beforeSendTransaction`, PERFORMANCE events). Both drop request headers/cookies, redact the request body, and run every URL through `scrubUrl` (public token segments: `/t/`, `/tip/`, `/unsubscribe/`, `/reset-password/`, `/api/voice/vm/`, plus `?token=`). Both hooks also scrub the transaction NAME, breadcrumbs, and the OpenTelemetry span attributes in `contexts.trace.data` (the transaction hook additionally walks `spans[].data` and span descriptions, which error events do not carry), and delete any captured `http.request.header.*` / `http.response.header.*` attribute (which re-carries what the request-block scrub removes). That span scrub works **by VALUE, not by key name**: every string attribute is run through `scrubUrl`, which is a no-op on anything lacking a public-token path shape. This is deliberate and load-bearing. A live listen token reached Sentry three times during the 2026-08-11 review. The first was a missing path shape plus an error-only hook; the last two were the same mistake twice — the defense was a list of attribute keys, and the SDK had one more. The third came through a bare `url` key that Sentry's own OTel bridge derives from `url.full` (`getRequestSpanData` in `@sentry/opentelemetry`), together with `http.query` and `http.fragment`; no key allowlist would have covered it. `SPAN_URL_KEYS` still exists but is documentation, pinned by a test asserting each listed key is genuinely scrubbed. `http.query` additionally gets a prefix-free `token=` rule because it is `url.search`, which carries the leading `?` in some SDK versions and not others. **When you add a public-token route, add its path shape to `scrubUrl`** — that one function is what both pipelines, every span attribute, and every breadcrumb go through. `scrubUrl` also redacts the Twilio Account SID, which is the basic-auth username on our media fetches and rides in outgoing-request URLs.
It also redacts the digits of an Inbox `p-` person key (an unknown number's last ten digits, in every `/api/admin/inbox/p-...` URL; case-insensitive, as Express routes), and the role-denial log (`logRoleDenial` in `server/middleware/auth.js`) sends its path through `scrubUrl` too, because the hooks never scrub `event.extra`.

The scrub fails CLOSED. Walking attributes we did not author means a frozen object or a throwing getter is reachable, and both must not end in a leak: the module is `'use strict'` (in sloppy mode a failed assignment is a SILENT no-op, which would ship the token with no symptom) and the walk is wrapped, because an exception escaping a Sentry hook makes the SDK DISCARD the whole event. An attribute bag that cannot be safely walked is therefore replaced wholesale with `{'sentry.scrub_failed': true}` and the event is tagged `scrub_failed=true`. **If you see that marker in Sentry, the span's attributes were dropped deliberately** — timing, `op`, and the span tree are intact; only the attribute bag is gone.
- **Client:** `@sentry/react` initialized in `client/src/index.js`. Gated on `REACT_APP_SENTRY_DSN_CLIENT`. `<ErrorBoundary>` captures unhandled React errors and forwards to Sentry. Every error event, transaction and breadcrumb first passes through `client/src/utils/sentryScrub.js` (`beforeSend`, `beforeSendTransaction`, `beforeBreadcrumb`): it walks every string, object keys included, and redacts an Inbox `p-` key (ten phone digits) the way the server's `scrubUrl` does, plus a bare `p-` key outside a path; it copies rather than mutates, and drops the item if the walk fails (spec 2026-10-06, section 17 item 42).
- **Webhook handlers** (Stripe, Resend, Thumbtack) wrap their `catch` blocks with explicit `Sentry.captureException` so processing errors land in Sentry while preserving the response codes those services expect.

### Special-case error routing

- **401 (session expired)** — toast (`'Your session expired — please log in again.'`) → 1.5s delay → context-aware redirect (staff `/login`, client `/client-login` on admin subdomain or `/login` on public site).
- **Network failure** (no response) — toast (`'Network error — check your connection.'`).
- **Stripe Elements card errors** — handled natively by the Stripe Element. Surrounding API failures (load invoice, create payment intent) use `<FormBanner>`.
- **Enumeration-sensitive endpoints** (forgot-password, client-login passwordless request) — always return success on the public response; `<FormBanner>` surfaces only hard errors (rate limit, server error).

## API Routes

Generated by `npm run docs` from a static parse of `server/index.js` and the routers it mounts (see `docs/dev.md` > Docs): the routes `server/index.js` registers itself first, then one table per mount statement in mount order, rows in registration order, each row's description the comment directly above its handler. The hand notes for each mount are under [Route notes](#route-notes) below.

<!-- gen:routes -->
Generated by npm run docs; do not edit between the markers.

### direct routes (server/index.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/files/:filename` | auth | server/index.js | Protected file access - admin and managers only. Returns a short-lived signed R2 URL in JSON rather than 302-redirecting, because a cross-origin XHR that follows a redirect to R2 trips CORS (R2 has no CORS headers for our origin) and surfaces as "Network error" in the client. The client opens the returned URL in a new tab (see AdminApplicationDetail / AdminUserDetail). |
| GET | `/api/health` | public | server/index.js | Health check - must be registered BEFORE the React catch-all below. Probes the DB (Render's health probe + uptime pingers hit this): a SELECT 1 raced against a ~2s timeout. DB reachable -> 200 {status:'ok'}; a DB error or a probe that outlives the timeout -> 503 {status:'degraded'} so the platform can pull an instance whose DB link has gone dark. |

### /api/auth/webauthn (server/routes/webauthn.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/auth/webauthn/register-options` | webauthnLimiter, auth | server/routes/webauthn.js | Registration options: resident key required (usernameless unlock), excludeCredentials from the caller's rows, challenge stored bound to the caller |
| POST | `/api/auth/webauthn/register-verify` | webauthnLimiter, auth | server/routes/webauthn.js | Verify attestation, insert `webauthn_credentials` row (label from body, 100 chars) |
| GET | `/api/auth/webauthn/credentials` | webauthnLimiter, auth | server/routes/webauthn.js | Caller's passkeys: id, label, created_at, last_used_at |
| DELETE | `/api/auth/webauthn/credentials/:id` | webauthnLimiter, auth | server/routes/webauthn.js | Revoke own passkey AND bump `users.token_version`: a deliberate global logout (lost-phone kill switch). The same transaction empties the user's stored push subscriptions (`adminPush.deleteAllSubscriptions`), so a lost phone stops buzzing |
| POST | `/api/auth/webauthn/assert-options` | public (webauthnLimiter) | server/routes/webauthn.js | Unlock options: empty allowCredentials (authenticator picks the resident passkey), unbound single-use challenge |
| POST | `/api/auth/webauthn/assert-verify` | public (webauthnLimiter) | server/routes/webauthn.js | Verify assertion (userHandle cross-check, atomic counter-advance claim, login's account-state gates), then mint the 12h token + login-shaped user payload |

### /api/auth (server/routes/auth.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/auth/register` | public (authLimiter) | server/routes/auth.js | Register |
| POST | `/api/auth/register-pre-hired` | public (authLimiter) | server/routes/auth.js | Register as a pre-hired contractor - open URL hand-off from admin (see docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-13-pre-hire-onboarding-design.md). Identical to POST /register except the new user has pre_hired=true so the application-submit handler can promote them to 'hired' (instead of 'applied') and seed contractor_profiles automatically - skipping the admin-review wait. |
| POST | `/api/auth/claim-pre-hire` | authLimiter, auth | server/routes/auth.js | Mark the current user as a pre-hired contractor. Used by the /onboarding landing page when an already-logged-in user visits it (returning recruit, or someone who registered at /register before being told about /onboarding). Handles three cases inside one transaction: - 'in_progress' (no application yet) → just set pre_hired=true - 'applied' (application submitted) → flip status to 'hired' AND seed contractor_profiles (same as POST /application would have done if pre_hired was true at submit time) AND write audit entries to interview_notes + application_activity - anything else (already 'hired'/'interviewing'/'rejected'/etc.) → no-op Rate-limited via authLimiter and gated to role='staff' (admins/managers have no business setting pre_hired on themselves). No emails fire - the recruit is already on /onboarding and lands on /welcome immediately; the admin gets the application_activity trail in lieu of an inbox notification, mirroring the choice in POST /application for pre_hired submitters. |
| POST | `/api/auth/login` | public (authLimiter) | server/routes/auth.js | Login |
| GET | `/api/auth/me` | auth | server/routes/auth.js | Get current user (includes has_application flag for routing) |
| POST | `/api/auth/forgot-password` | public (authLimiter) | server/routes/auth.js | Forgot password - request reset link |
| POST | `/api/auth/reset-password` | public (authLimiter) | server/routes/auth.js | Reset password - set new password with token |

### /api/admin/thumbtack (server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/admin/thumbtack/pending-harvest` | agentLimiter, agentSecretOnly | server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js | GET /api/admin/thumbtack/pending-harvest?limit=N - agent-secret only. Returns up to N { negotiation_id } for pending, email-null, past-cooldown clients that still have a non-terminal Thumbtack lead, and atomically LEASES each (stamps email_harvest_attempted_at=now()) in the SAME statement (FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED) so an overlapping poll can never re-hand the same lead. The lease does NOT touch email_harvest_attempts (the failure counter, bumped only by harvest-failed). negotiation_id is the latest NON-terminal lead. Kill-switch: HARVESTER_ENABLED='false' returns [] (a redeploy-free stop, even if the box is unreachable). |
| POST | `/api/admin/thumbtack/email-harvested` | agentLimiter, agentOrAdmin | server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js | POST /api/admin/thumbtack/email-harvested { negotiation_id, email } Agent-secret OR admin/manager JWT. Sets clients.email + status 'harvested'. The AGENT path may only write a pending+null client (a leaked secret cannot stamp arbitrary rows); the ADMIN manual-paste path may override any status. A UNIQUE-email collision is terminal + alert on the AGENT path, but a recoverable 409 on the ADMIN path (human typo or real duplicate). NEVER auto-merges, NEVER stamps on collision. |
| POST | `/api/admin/thumbtack/rearm` | agentLimiter, auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js | POST /api/admin/thumbtack/rearm { negotiation_id } - admin/manager JWT ONLY (no agent-secret path). Puts a FAILED lead back in the agent queue: status 'pending', attempts 0, attempted_at NULL (clears the cooldown so the agent re-picks it). |
| POST | `/api/admin/thumbtack/harvest-failed` | agentLimiter, agentSecretOnly | server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js | POST /api/admin/thumbtack/harvest-failed { negotiation_id, reason } - agent-secret only. |
| GET | `/api/admin/thumbtack/pending-first-replies` | agentLimiter, agentSecretOnly | server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js | GET /api/admin/thumbtack/pending-first-replies?limit=N (agent-secret only) Reply work queue. ONE writable CTE (the pending-harvest shape): pick pending, past-cooldown rows FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED; the UPDATE stamps the lease AND bumps the attempts counter in the same statement. Offer-side rules: - night rows created in the Chicago dead-hours window (default 2am-8am, FIRST_REPLY_NIGHT_JITTER_START_HOUR/_END_HOUR) are withheld until created_at + (2 + id % 13) minutes so dead-of-night replies land minutes-spread instead of on a constant beat; all other night rows and all day rows offer immediately (call ordering dominates); - a day row offered while LEAD_CALL_ENABLED='false' is downgraded to night IN the DB before offering (never promise a call we will not place); - rows at the attempts cap flip to 'failed', and the final SELECT filters to rows still 'pending', so a cap-flipped row is NEVER handed to the agent; - rows older than the call freshness bound are never offered (a weeks-old "expect my call" reply after a flag-off/on cycle would be worse than no reply); the 60s sweep retires such strands to 'failed'. Kill switch: anything but TT_AUTOREPLY_ENABLED='true' returns [] (feature ships dark; default OFF, unlike HARVESTER_ENABLED's default ON). |
| POST | `/api/admin/thumbtack/first-reply-sent` | agentLimiter, agentSecretOnly | server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js | POST /api/admin/thumbtack/first-reply-sent { negotiation_id, template } Agent-secret only. Guarded pending->sent flip; the flip WINNER with a DB template of 'day' fires the promised call via skipWindowCheck (the window was judged at lead arrival; kill switch, config, phone validation, and the cap still apply inside the trigger). The DB first_reply_template is the source of truth: the posted template is logged on mismatch, never trusted, so a forged body cannot influence the dial decision. |
| POST | `/api/admin/thumbtack/first-reply-failed` | agentLimiter, agentSecretOnly | server/routes/thumbtackAgent.js | POST /api/admin/thumbtack/first-reply-failed { negotiation_id, reason } Agent-secret only. Definitive agent failures flip pending->failed. The reason is validated and logged, NOT stored: no reason column in v1 (spec 4.6 surfaces status only). Transient troubles are never reported; the lease cooldown re-offers and the offer-side attempts cap bounds them. No email. |

### /api/admin/packages (server/routes/packages.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/admin/packages/` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | GET /api/admin/packages - the ladder, retired included, with item counts. |
| GET | `/api/admin/packages/:id(\d+)` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | GET /api/admin/packages/:id - package + its contents rows. |
| PUT | `/api/admin/packages/:id(\d+)` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | PUT /api/admin/packages/:id - slots config + active flag ONLY. Pricing columns stay admin-dashboard/SQL territory; this surface is contents. |
| POST | `/api/admin/packages/:id(\d+)/items` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | POST /api/admin/packages/:id/items |
| PUT | `/api/admin/packages/:id(\d+)/items/:itemId(\d+)` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | PUT /api/admin/packages/:id/items/:itemId |
| DELETE | `/api/admin/packages/:id(\d+)/items/:itemId(\d+)` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | DELETE /api/admin/packages/:id/items/:itemId |
| GET | `/api/admin/packages/:id(\d+)/makeability` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | GET /api/admin/packages/:id/makeability - every active drink classified against the package: covered / fenced (+$/guest) / unmakeable / no_recipe. Recomputed live by the editor after any contents or recipe edit. |
| GET | `/api/admin/packages/:id(\d+)/margin` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/packages.js | GET /api/admin/packages/:id/margin?guests=&hours=&labor=&supplies= Directional, not accounting: retires the pricing spreadsheet, never bills anyone. Revenue uses the package's own per-guest rates; liquor cost uses category pars x the MEAN cost of eligible bottles (split pars share the category volume, so extra "for show" labels never multiply cost). |

Use only when the existing public URL is compromised (printed QR card was photographed, screenshot leaked, etc.). Customers with the old QR can no longer pay through it. In-flight Stripe sessions on the old link will fail or, if completed in the brief gap, get dropped at the webhook because the metadata token won't match the rotated DB token. |
| POST | `/api/admin/contractors/:userId/tip-page/regenerate-stripe` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/admin/contractorTipPage.js | POST - rotate the Stripe Payment Link (deactivate old, create new). Token unchanged. |
| POST | `/api/admin/contractors/:userId/tip-page/generate-stripe` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/admin/contractorTipPage.js | POST - create a Stripe link when one is missing (and ensure a token exists). Fails 409 if a link already exists - forces an explicit regenerate call. |
| POST | `/api/admin/contractors/:userId/tip-page/deactivate` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/admin/contractorTipPage.js | POST - disable the page + deactivate Stripe link |
| POST | `/api/admin/contractors/:userId/tip-page/activate` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/admin/contractorTipPage.js | POST - re-enable the page + reactivate Stripe link |

| POST | `/api/admin/tip-feedback/:id/review` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/contractorTipPage.js | Mark feedback reviewed (admin-only - mirrors the feedback view's gate) |

| GET | `/api/admin/applications/:userId` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/applications.js | Get single application detail with scorecard + unified timeline. Shape preserves the legacy { user, application, notes } keys (read by the soon-to-retire AdminApplicationDetail.js) and adds scorecard + timeline used by the rebuilt detail page. |
| POST | `/api/admin/applications/:userId/notes` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/applications.js | Add interview note. Writes to BOTH interview_notes (legacy reads still work) AND application_activity (so the new timeline picks it up live without waiting on a refetch). Both writes share a transaction - symmetric with the other multi-write endpoints below. |
| DELETE | `/api/admin/notes/:noteId` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/applications.js | Delete interview note |

| DELETE | `/api/admin/applications/:userId/interview` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/applications.js | Clear a scheduled interview (returns applicant to "unscheduled" state). |

Also cannot come from the applications list, which INNER JOINs applications and so omits the direct hires this is for. |

Proposal and event rows also carry `status`, `event_date` (YYYY-MM-DD or null) and `kind` (the event-type label) for the phone search (lane ma-f3); the palette reads only `type, id, name, detail`. |

| PATCH | `/api/admin/payroll/payout-events/:id` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js |  |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/periods/:id/process` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js | Flip `open`/`reopened` → `processing`: runs the fee-recapture healing pass, freezes line edits. 409s while the period's week is still in progress unless body `{ force: true }` (early-process wage guard). Finalizes immediately when no pending payouts remain; response carries `period_status` (`'paid'` or `'processing'`) plus the `fee_recapture` summary. |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/periods/:id/reopen` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js | The "I fucked up" path: flip `processing` → `reopened` (never back to `open`) so PENDING payouts' lines become editable again while every payroll money writer still refuses the period. Paid payouts stay locked. 409 unless the period is `processing`; audit-logged as `payroll_period_reopen`. |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/payouts/:id/mark-paid` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js | Record an external payment: `{ payment_method, payment_handle, payment_reference?, expected_total_cents? }`. `zelle` is an allowed method. `expected_total_cents` (non-negative integer) is the drift guard: compared against `total_cents` under the row lock, 409 on mismatch so a stale generated QR/link can never be recorded. 409s a `no_draw` payout (convert to pending first). |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/payouts/:id/toggle-draw` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js | Flip a payout between 'pending' (owed, payable) and 'no_draw' (owner row: tracked, never owed). Spec 2026-08-07. Guards mirror mark-paid: row-locked, unpaid only, period not 'paid'. Parking the LAST pending payout of a processing period runs the same finalize check as mark-paid so the period closes; un-parking in a processing period makes the pay panel live again. |
| GET | `/api/admin/payroll/unassigned-tips` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js |  |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/payroll/tips/:id/assign` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js |  |
| GET | `/api/admin/payroll/deferred-tips` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js | List tips deferred while the open pay period was frozen (with stuck-reason: `frozen_period`, `stubs`, or `max_attempts`). |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/deferred-tips/retry` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js | Manually run the deferred-tip sweep; audit-logged as `payroll_deferred_tips_retry` with the summary in `metadata`. |
| GET | `/api/admin/payroll/contractors/:userId/payouts` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payroll.js |  |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/duty-lines` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollDuty.js | POST /payroll/duty-lines - admin manual line. |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/payroll/duty-lines/:id` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollDuty.js | PATCH /payroll/duty-lines/:id - amount and note edits stamp admin_owned and confirm a held line (the admin decision, mirroring the payout-event PATCH). |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/duty-lines/:id/remove` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollDuty.js | POST /payroll/duty-lines/:id/remove and /restore - the pay-time checkbox. Restore keeps the stored amount (no re-derive). An admin removal is FINAL to derivation: reconcile never resurrects removed_by IS NOT NULL rows. |
| POST | `/api/admin/payroll/duty-lines/:id/restore` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollDuty.js | POST /payroll/duty-lines/:id/remove and /restore - the pay-time checkbox. Restore keeps the stored amount (no re-derive). An admin removal is FINAL to derivation: reconcile never resurrects removed_by IS NOT NULL rows. |
| PUT | `/api/admin/payroll/duty-attributions` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollDuty.js | PUT /payroll/duty-attributions - set or move who gets an attributed duty. Validates against the CURRENT approved roster inside the transaction, then re-derives through the one shared code path (accrual), which materializes the new line, system-removes the old contractor's, and recomputes both totals. Derivation runs AFTER release on its own connection (accrual manages its own transaction; one-pooled-connection rule). |
| GET | `/api/admin/payroll/periods/:id/unattributed-duties` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollDuty.js | GET /payroll/periods/:id/unattributed-duties - what blocks Process, plus display names for the modal. |
| GET | `/api/admin/payroll/contractors/:userId/payment-history` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollTax.js | GET /api/admin/payroll/contractors/:userId/payment-history All-time imported payment history for one contractor + a blended all-time total (imported ledger + PAID OS payouts). staff_payment_history ONLY. |
| GET | `/api/admin/payroll/tax-totals` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollTax.js | GET /api/admin/payroll/tax-totals?year=2026 Per-contractor calendar-year totals for 1099 season. Ledger grouped by paid_on year; payouts grouped by paid_at year (constructive receipt, spec §4 - never payday). A user appears if they have EITHER a ledger row OR a paid payout in the year (union of the two id sets = the "full outer on user" behaviour the spec calls for). platforms = per-platform ledger cents. |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/payroll/tax-totals/:userId/exclude` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/payrollTax.js | PATCH /api/admin/payroll/tax-totals/:userId/exclude Toggle the per-person 1099 exclusion flag (foreign contractors on W-8BEN, e.g. Zul). Admin-toggleable post-import - not set-once. |
| GET | `/api/admin/staff-reviews/leaderboard` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/staffReviewsContest.js | GET /staff-reviews/leaderboard?quarter=2026-Q3 |
| POST | `/api/admin/staff-reviews/contest-award` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/staffReviewsContest.js | POST /staff-reviews/contest-award {quarter} - one-click, never automatic. Idempotent through UNIQUE(contest_quarter, contractor_id): a second click returns the existing rows and creates nothing (spec §7). |
| GET | `/api/admin/staff-reviews` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/staffReviews.js | GET /staff-reviews - pending first, then newest. Credits ride the payload. |
| POST | `/api/admin/staff-reviews` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/staffReviews.js | POST /staff-reviews - manual Google row. Google reviews carry no external key, so dedup is admin judgment: the response flags a same-date Thumbtack row rather than refusing (spec §7). |
| PATCH | `/api/admin/staff-reviews/:id` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/staffReviews.js | PATCH /staff-reviews/:id - edit the review AND set its credits (replace-style). Un-crediting a staffer whose bounty already materialized: system-remove the line while the period is open; once frozen, alert only and never write (spec §7 / §3.2). |
| POST | `/api/admin/staff-reviews/:id/confirm` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/staffReviews.js | POST /staff-reviews/:id/confirm - the money moment. One held client, one transaction: flip the status, materialize each credited staffer's bounty (5 stars only), then run the catch-up pass for anything that was waiting on an open period. |
| POST | `/api/admin/staff-reviews/:id/dismiss` | auth, adminOnly | server/routes/admin/staffReviews.js | POST /staff-reviews/:id/dismiss - refuse while any bounty for this review is paid or frozen; otherwise system-remove the open ones and dismiss. |
| GET | `/api/admin/staff-hub/summary` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/admin/staffHub.js |  |

| POST | `/api/admin/presence/leads` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/admin/presence.js | Set own taking-leads toggle; rejected while away. The fallback owner's toggle is dibs: it overrides the chain, and both edges fire the dibs ping (`presenceNotify.js`). |

### /api/shifts (server/routes/shifts.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/shifts/` | auth, requireOnboarded | server/routes/shifts.js | GET /shifts - open upcoming shifts for staff; all shifts for admin/manager |
| GET | `/api/shifts/unstaffed-upcoming` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | GET /shifts/unstaffed-upcoming - admin-facing list of upcoming shifts that still need staffing. Smaller than the full /shifts dump and pre-filtered server-side so the AssignToEventModal can render without fetching ~500 rows. The roster is read by openSlotsSql, the same fragment badge-counts uses; it casts positions_needed to jsonb only behind IS JSON ARRAY. |
| GET | `/api/shifts/user/:userId/events` | auth | server/routes/shifts.js | GET /shifts/user/:userId/events - event history for a user (staff or admin) |
| GET | `/api/shifts/my-requests` | auth | server/routes/shifts.js | GET /shifts/my-requests - current user's shift history (with team for approved) |
| GET | `/api/shifts/by-proposal/:proposalId` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | GET /shifts/by-proposal/:proposalId - return every shift attached to a proposal as an array. A single proposal can spawn multiple shifts (e.g. multi-day events). Avoids the EventDetailPage shipping the full /shifts list to the browser just to filter for one proposal. LIMIT 100 is defensive - a single event with >100 shifts is unheard of, but it bounds the worst case if a future bug ever creates a runaway loop. |
| GET | `/api/shifts/detail/:id` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | GET /shifts/detail/:id - single shift details (admin/manager only) |
| PATCH | `/api/shifts/:id/out-of-area` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | PATCH /shifts/:id/out-of-area - attach, raise, lower, or clear the Out-of-Area Bonus on a shift (spec §6). Body: `{ amount_cents: int\|null }`, cents, `0 < amount <= 25000` (the DB CHECK carries the same bound); null clears an UNLOCKED bonus. |
| POST | `/api/shifts/:id/request` | auth, requireOnboarded | server/routes/shifts.js | POST /shifts/:id/request - staff requests to work a shift (ranked roles + transport ack). Position is resolved at approval, not here. See shifts.approval.js. |
| DELETE | `/api/shifts/requests/:requestId` | auth | server/routes/shifts.js | DELETE /shifts/requests/:requestId - staff withdraws their own request (pending-only); staffing (an admin, or a manager with can_staff) can delete any status. A manager WITHOUT can_staff is a staffer here: their own pending request and nothing else. |
| PUT | `/api/shifts/:id` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | PUT /shifts/:id - update a shift. Body lives in shifts.handlers.js. |
| DELETE | `/api/shifts/:id` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | DELETE /shifts/:id - delete a shift |
| POST | `/api/shifts/:id/cancel-or-unassign` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | POST /shifts/:id/cancel-or-unassign - first-class cancel / unassign action. Body lives in shifts.handlers.js. |

| GET | `/api/shifts/:id/requests` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | GET /shifts/:id/requests - get all requests for a shift |
| PUT | `/api/shifts/requests/:requestId` | auth, requireStaffing | server/routes/shifts.js | PUT /shifts/requests/:requestId - approve or deny a request. On approval, position is resolved from the staffer's ranked requested_positions or an admin override (the money seam); see shifts.approval.js. |

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |

Returns the list only when admin has explicitly approved it. While the list is auto-generated and waiting for review (status='pending_review'), or no list exists yet, the response stays in the "being prepared" placeholder state so clients don't see unreviewed quantities. |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/t/:token/lab-cta` | requireUuidToken(...), publicReadLimiter | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | POST /api/drink-plans/t/:token/lab-cta - celebration-screen Enhancement Lab CTA click marker (attach-rate funnel; first click wins). Public, token-gated, write-once, no body. |

Body: { addOns: {slug: {servingStyle?, toastTime?, drinks?, flavors?}}, labSyrupSelections: {drinkId: [syrupId]} } - the DESIRED lab state (idempotent; removing an addition = omitting it). Non-lab addOns entries (planner fence picks, legacy) are never touched. |

Handler extracted to ./drinkPlans/submit.js (file-size cap); the requireUuidToken guard + drinkPlanWriteLimiter stay here on the mount. |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/t/:token/logo` | requireUuidToken(...), logoUploadLimiter | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | POST /api/drink-plans/t/:token/logo Public token-gated logo upload. Accepts multipart with field 'logo'. Validates magic bytes + size + extension. Uploads to R2 under drink-plan-logos/<plan-id>-<timestamp>.<ext>. Atomically merges the URL + filename into selections.companyLogo via Postgres jsonb \|\| operator. Returns { logoUrl, selections }. |
| GET | `/api/drink-plans/t/:token/logo` | requireUuidToken(...), logoUploadLimiter | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | GET /api/drink-plans/t/:token/logo Public token-gated logo proxy. Returns the R2 object bytes with the appropriate content-type so both the client preview (unauthenticated token-gated context) and the admin event detail page (and html2canvas during PNG export) can fetch the image from a same-origin URL. |
| GET | `/api/drink-plans/` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | GET /api/drink-plans - list all plans. Exclude selections/shopping_list JSONB blobs (each 100 KB+). Detail endpoint returns selections; shopping_list has its own route. package_category rides along so the overview prep queue can skip hosted plans (a hosted package never owes a shopping list; see shoppingListGen.isHostedPlan). Paginated via ?limit (default 200, max 500) + ?offset to keep the response bounded as the table grows. |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | POST /api/drink-plans - create a new plan |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/for-proposal/:proposalId` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | POST /api/drink-plans/for-proposal/:proposalId - create a drink plan for a proposal (admin) |
| GET | `/api/drink-plans/by-proposal/:proposalId` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | GET /api/drink-plans/by-proposal/:proposalId - fetch plan by proposal id. Mirrors the GET /:id projection (consult + shopping-list status flags, kept as IS NOT NULL booleans so the JSONB blobs stay off the wire) so the event-page DrinkPlanCard can drive consult + shopping-list controls without a second round-trip. selections is kept (needed for detail); shopping_list itself has its own endpoint. |
| GET | `/api/drink-plans/:id` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | GET /api/drink-plans/:id - fetch single plan by id. Exclude shopping_list (has its own endpoint); keep selections for detail rendering. Booleans (`has_consult_selections`, `has_shopping_list`) keep the JSONB blobs off the wire - the consult payload itself is only fetched on demand from GET /:id/consult when the form is opened. |
| PATCH | `/api/drink-plans/:id/notes` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | PATCH /api/drink-plans/:id/notes - update admin notes |
| PATCH | `/api/drink-plans/:id/status` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, drinkPlanWriteLimiter | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | PATCH /api/drink-plans/:id/status - update plan status. A plain setter since 2026-09-22 (approving the shopping list is the review and the only derived-finalize trigger; nothing in the UI sends 'reviewed' any more). Carries the drink-plan write limiter like finalize/unfinalize. The finalize lock lives INSIDE the UPDATE (not a pre-check): an approve in another tab can finalize the plan between a check and the write, and a status change must never land on a finalized plan. Zero rows on an existing plan is that lock. |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/:id/finalize` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, drinkPlanWriteLimiter | server/utils/beoFinalize.js |  |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/:id/unfinalize` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, drinkPlanWriteLimiter | server/utils/beoFinalize.js | Clear `finalized_at`/`finalized_by`, suppress pending nudges (preserves sent), clear every `beo_acknowledged_at` on the proposal's shift_requests, write `beo_unfinalized` activity log. A plan the client never submitted (`submitted_at` NULL, finalized off an admin-built list) goes back to `draft` so the public planner reopens; a client-submitted plan stays `reviewed`. |
| GET | `/api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans/shoppingList.js | GET /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list - load saved shopping list |
| PUT | `/api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans/shoppingList.js | PUT /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list - save/update shopping list. Keeps the list in `pending_review` until the admin explicitly approves; an admin re-edit of an already-approved list reverts it to pending so the client doesn't keep reading stale numbers. |
| PATCH | `/api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list/approve` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans/shoppingList.js | PATCH /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list/approve - DEPRECATED direct approve, kept mounted for API compatibility. Delegates to the shopping_list_approve comms action (spec 4.4): idempotent side effects (atomic flip + approved snapshot), then the default unedited email on the email channel, awaited and ledgered by the action, never fire-and-forget. New UI goes through POST /api/comms/send instead. |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/:id/logo` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | POST /api/drink-plans/:id/logo Admin-authenticated logo upload by plan ID. Same validation + R2 upload + atomic JSONB merge as the token-gated route. |
| DELETE | `/api/drink-plans/:id/logo` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | DELETE /api/drink-plans/:id/logo Admin-authenticated. Clears selections.companyLogo. Does NOT delete the R2 file (storage cost is negligible; no cleanup job in v1). |
| DELETE | `/api/drink-plans/:id` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | DELETE /api/drink-plans/:id - delete a plan |
| POST | `/api/drink-plans/:id/resend-nudge` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, adminWriteLimiter | server/routes/drinkPlans.js | POST /api/drink-plans/:id/resend-nudge - DEPRECATED direct resend, kept mounted for API compatibility. Delegates to the drink_plan_nudge comms action (spec 4.4): the action resolves the LIVE client email + phone via the proposal join, enforces the archived block (ensureSideEffects), honors the SMS opt-out / bad-phone gate through channel availability, and OWNS its ledger writes (sent + failed) so a provider throw can never leave a sent-but-unlogged message. This is the admin "Resend planner link" button; unlike the scheduled T-21 nudge it does NOT suppress on an already-filled plan (deliberate admin override). Links use the DRINK-PLAN token, which is what /plan/:token resolves. New UI goes through POST /api/comms/send instead. |

### /api/proposals (server/routes/proposals/index.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/proposals/t/:token/resolve` | requireUuidToken, publicTokenIpLimiter, proposalCheckoutLimiter | server/routes/proposals/publicToken.js | GET /api/proposals/t/:token/resolve - NON-mutating. Tells the client whether this proposal is one option in a comparison group, and whether the group is already decided, so ProposalView can redirect to /compare/:token WITHOUT bumping view_count or flipping sent->viewed (which the full GET below does - merely landing on a link that will be bounced must not inflate that option's engagement). |
| GET | `/api/proposals/t/:token/payment-state` | requireUuidToken, publicTokenIpLimiter, proposalPollLimiter | server/routes/proposals/publicToken.js | GET /api/proposals/t/:token/payment-state - NON-mutating. The poll target for the post-checkout settle state (spec 2026-08-28 §3c). Returns only the row's payment state, in dollars, and touches NOTHING: no view_count bump, no last_viewed_at, no sent->viewed flip, no activity row. The full GET does all four on every call, and a thirteen-attempt poll against it would record thirteen views of a page the client is staring at once. 404 on archived is stated here on its own; /resolve above is deliberately status-blind. |
| GET | `/api/proposals/t/:token` | requireUuidToken, publicTokenIpLimiter, proposalCheckoutLimiter | server/routes/proposals/publicToken.js |  |

When decided, `decided` + `chosen_token` tell the client to route to the booked option. publicReadLimiter (100/15min): read-only, and it shares a client session with proposal loads + the explore matrix's calculate batch - the 20/15min publicLimiter budget would 429 normal compare sessions. |
| GET | `/api/proposals/group/:token/preview` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, requireUuidToken(...) | server/routes/proposals/compareGroup.js | GET /api/proposals/group/:token/preview - admin preview; ignores the visibility gate so admin can review the comparison (including draft options) before sending. Each option carries client_visible so the preview can name the ones the client cannot see yet; the public payload above never needs it (every option is visible). |

Write endpoints in this file (capture-lead, submit) stay on publicLimiter. |

POST in verb but read-only in effect (pure pricing preview, no write), so it sits on publicReadLimiter (100/15min) - see the packages note above; the explore matrix fires one of these per non-class package in parallel. |
| POST | `/api/proposals/public/capture-lead` | public (publicLimiter) | server/routes/proposals/public.js | POST /api/proposals/public/capture-lead - capture partial lead from quote wizard + create draft |
| GET | `/api/proposals/public/quote-draft/:token` | public (publicReadLimiter) | server/routes/proposals/public.js | GET /api/proposals/public/quote-draft/:token - fetch saved draft for resume |
| PUT | `/api/proposals/public/quote-draft/:token` | public (publicReadLimiter) | server/routes/proposals/public.js | PUT /api/proposals/public/quote-draft/:token - auto-save draft state |
| POST | `/api/proposals/public/submit` | public (publicLimiter) | server/routes/proposals/public.js | POST /api/proposals/public/submit - create a proposal from the public website quote wizard |
| GET | `/api/proposals/packages` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/metadata.js | GET /api/proposals/packages - list active packages |
| GET | `/api/proposals/addons` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/metadata.js | GET /api/proposals/addons - list active add-ons |
| POST | `/api/proposals/calculate` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/metadata.js | POST /api/proposals/calculate - preview pricing without saving. Mirrors the POST / persist path: add-ons run through stripIncludedAddons (bundle-covered add-ons dropped) and carry a bounded quantity, so the preview total matches what the proposal would actually be saved at. |
| GET | `/api/proposals/financials` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/metadata.js | GET /api/proposals/financials - aggregate financial data |
| GET | `/api/proposals/:id/lead-cost` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/metadata.js | GET /api/proposals/:id/lead-cost - Thumbtack acquisition cost for one proposal. The charged (or legacy NULL-state priced) lead linked via thumbtack_leads.proposal_id, stamped by the auto-draft. Informational, read-only; drives the "Acquisition" line on the admin payment panel. Non-numeric / unlinked ids resolve to { leadCost: null } (never a 500). |
| GET | `/api/proposals/dashboard-stats` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/metadata.js | GET /api/proposals/dashboard-stats - aggregates that the admin home dashboard renders. Server-side so totals stay accurate past the 50-row default LIMIT on /api/proposals. A `source` query param (thumbtack\|manual) returns ONLY { pipeline, paidCount, archivedCount } (the tab-count subset) - the full-KPI cards never pass `source`. |
| PATCH | `/api/proposals/:id/status` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, adminWriteLimiter | server/routes/proposals/lifecycle.js | PATCH /api/proposals/:id/status - update status. Enforce state machine unless ?force=true (admin-only) |
| POST | `/api/proposals/:id/resend` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, adminWriteLimiter | server/routes/proposals/lifecycle.js | POST /api/proposals/:id/resend - DEPRECATED direct resend, kept mounted for API compatibility. Delegates to the proposal_resend comms action (plan P1): validate-only side effects (the resendable-status guard; status/timestamps stay untouched, as before), then the default unedited proposalSent email + initial-proposal SMS on the action's default channels, awaited and ledgered by the action (skipLog inside), never fire-and-forget. The action honors the legacy SMS suppression (opted-out / bad phone -> SMS unavailable). New UI goes through POST /api/comms/send instead. A 'resent' activity row still records it. |
| POST | `/api/proposals/:id/portal-invite` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, adminWriteLimiter | server/routes/proposals/lifecycle.js | POST /api/proposals/:id/portal-invite - DEPRECATED direct portal invite, kept mounted for API compatibility. Delegates to the portal_invite comms action (plan P1): no state side effect (the OTP portal mints no token), then the default unedited invite email on the action's default (email-only) channel, awaited and ledgered by the action. New UI goes through POST /api/comms/send. The activity log is secondary and never 500s an already-sent invite. |
| PATCH | `/api/proposals/:id/notes` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/actions.js | PATCH /api/proposals/:id/notes - update admin notes |
| POST | `/api/proposals/:id/create-shift` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/actions.js | POST /api/proposals/:id/create-shift - manually create event shift from a proposal |
| PATCH | `/api/proposals/:id/balance-due-date` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/actions.js | PATCH /api/proposals/:id/balance-due-date - override balance due date |
| POST | `/api/proposals/:id/send-reminder` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/actions.js | POST /api/proposals/:id/send-reminder - DEPRECATED direct balance reminder, kept mounted for API compatibility. Delegates to the payment_reminder comms action (plan P1): validate-only side effects (the NO_BALANCE_DUE guard; no money column is written), then the default unedited balance-reminder email + SMS on the action's default channels, awaited and ledgered by the action (per-channel truth, so a failed channel no longer 502s - it returns email/sms:'failed' + error). New UI goes through POST /api/comms/send. A 'reminder_sent' activity row still records it. |
| POST | `/api/proposals/:id/record-payment` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/actions.js | POST /api/proposals/:id/record-payment - manually record an outside payment (cash, Venmo, etc.) |
| POST | `/api/proposals/:id/archive` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/actions.js | POST /api/proposals/:id/archive - archive this proposal, or (scope 'set') this plus every other open, unpaid proposal for the same client (covers formal option groups and loose multi-proposal sets with one rule). Archives in one transaction with unpaid-invoice voids; best-effort marketing/change-request reaps run post-commit, matching the ->archived lifecycle semantics. |

Looks up by id with no is_active filter - a historical request may reference a since-deactivated package and its name must still render. |
| POST | `/api/proposals/change-requests/:id/decline` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/changeRequests.js | POST /api/proposals/change-requests/:id/decline, decline with a required reason. |

| GET | `/api/proposals/:id/group` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/proposals/groups.js | GET /api/proposals/:id/group - group summary for the admin Alternatives panel. { grouped:false } when the proposal is solo. |

Delegates to the proposal_send_group comms action (plan P1): ensureSideEffects runs the exact groupSend transaction (FOR UPDATE lock, transition every draft option to 'sent', deferred invoicing, suppressed per-option comms) idempotently; dispatch then sends the single proposalOptionsSent compare email (email only), but only when something was newly sent - mirroring groupSend's dedupe. New UI goes through POST /api/comms/send. |

Zero notices when `change_request_id` is present. Writes nothing. |

Keyed by the proposal so an event with no shift row can still be read; the traversal guard and the R2 stream are the staff route's, shared through menuPrintFile.js. beoReadLimiter is the staff route's per-user read budget: every call presigns and streams up to 10 MB from R2, and a human never comes near 60 in 15 min. |

### /api/sms (server/routes/sms.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/sms/inbound` | public (inboundLimiter) | server/routes/sms.js | POST /api/sms/inbound - Twilio inbound-message webhook. No JWT (provider webhook; authenticity comes from the Twilio signature). 403 on bad/missing signature; 500 on unexpected error (Twilio retries - safe, processInboundSms dedupes on MessageSid); 200 with TwiML on every handled outcome. |
| POST | `/api/sms/status` | public (statusLimiter) | server/routes/sms.js | POST /api/sms/status: Twilio's message status callback (spec 2026-10-06, Inbox, section 9), registered only by Messages-page replies and Inbox sends (smsStatusCallbackUrl()). Signature-verified exactly like /inbound: 403 in production, warn and allow in dev. It records exactly two things: - error 21610 writes sms_optouts for the recipient (decision 10), first, so a failure in the row bookkeeping never skips it; - failed or undelivered flips the matching OUTBOUND row (by twilio_sid) to status 'failed', with Twilio's numeric error code in error_message. A failure whose sid matches no row yet (the callback beat the sender's INSERT, which runs after Twilio answers) is kept in sms_status_orphans for the Inbox reader, unless the sid belongs to an inbound row; once it is stored, the flip runs once more, so a reply row inserted in between (before its own fold could see the orphan) still ends up failed. Every other status is ignored and never stored: prod's status CHECK rejects 'accepted', and queued, sent or delivered change nothing a reply depends on. It does not touch clients.phone_status (fix list). 204 when handled; 500 on a DB error. |
| GET | `/api/sms/conversations` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/sms.js | GET /api/sms/conversations - one row per client that has any SMS. Threads with an unread inbound come first; within each block, ordered by the client's most recent inbound (received) message, newest first. Threads a client never replied to (no inbound) sort last. Includes an unread inbound count. |
| GET | `/api/sms/conversations/:clientId` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/sms.js | GET /api/sms/conversations/:clientId - full message thread, oldest first. |
| POST | `/api/sms/conversations/:clientId/reply` | auth, requireAdminOrManager, adminWriteLimiter | server/routes/sms.js | POST /api/sms/conversations/:clientId/reply: send an outbound SMS to the client and log it. Body: { body }. |
| PUT | `/api/sms/conversations/:clientId/read` | auth, requireAdminOrManager | server/routes/sms.js | PUT /api/sms/conversations/:clientId/read - mark every unread inbound message for this client as read. |

| POST | `/api/voice/consult/digit` | public | server/routes/voiceConsultCall.js | POST /digit?attempt&leg&ring&play - Gather action. 1 = claim the bridge and dial the booker; 9 = replay (max 3 plays); anything else = hang up and let the status callback advance the chain. |

One of MISSED_DIAL_STATUSES means the client did not pick up, which is the one thing the agent on the line cannot tell from a Twilio status: latch it, read the number back so he can try again, then text Dallas once. Everything else, 'completed' included, hangs up silently. |

### /api/voice (server/routes/voice.js)

| Method | Path | Auth | Handler | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/voice/inbound` | (inline) | server/routes/voice.js | POST /api/voice/inbound - a client calls the 224. Dial Zul's cell (VA_CELL), passing the client's number through as caller ID so she sees who is calling. VA_CELL is a strict-E.164 env var (never normalized). The client From is external input interpolated into the callerId ATTRIBUTE; xmlEscape does not escape double-quotes, so From is first constrained to a bare +digits E.164 shape (safe by construction) and otherwise falls back to VOICE_CALLER_ID. Both interpolated values are still xmlEscape'd defensively. The <Dial> carries a hard timeLimit so a forwarded inbound leg can never bill unbounded PH minutes. |
| POST | `/api/voice/inbound/primary` | (inline) | server/routes/voice.js | POST /api/voice/inbound/primary. a client calls the 1922, the primary business number. Dial Dallas (VM_PRIMARY_DIAL_TARGET, normally the 312 that rings his phone and keeps his personal cell private), pass the client's number through as caller ID, and hand a miss to the shared voicemail handler with line=primary. Fail-closed on signature: billed legs downstream. |
| POST | `/api/voice/bridge` | public | server/routes/voice.js | POST /api/voice/bridge - fetched after Zul answers her outbound leg. The dial target is looked up FROM pending_call BY CallSid (never a request param, so a forged param cannot redirect the second leg). Unknown/expired CallSid → a spoken apology + hangup rather than a silent dead call. Provider webhook: any error returns a valid TwiML (never a stack trace) so the call terminates cleanly. |
| POST | `/api/voice/status` | public | server/routes/voice.js | POST /api/voice/status - Twilio call-status callback. On a dead leg (no-answer/busy/failed/canceled) message Zul so she learns the outcome instead of hearing silence, and audit the status. Always returns an empty 204 (Twilio needs a 2xx to stop retrying; side-effect failures are logged, never surfaced). CallSid/status redaction: audit stores full CallSid but no dialed number here. |
| POST | `/api/voice/inbound/missed` | public (voicemailWebhookLimiter) | server/routes/voice.js | POST /api/voice/inbound/missed - the <Dial> action callback. Twilio requests this when the dialed leg ends, whatever the outcome. |
| POST | `/api/voice/inbound/voicemail` | public (voicemailWebhookLimiter) | server/routes/voice.js | The claim is awaited BEFORE the 204, then the slow work runs detached. Answering 204 first would leave a crash window (SIGTERM between the response and the claim) where the row stays 'missed' with a NULL recording_sid, which the sweep's `recording_sid IS NOT NULL` filter cannot reach and Twilio will never retry. The claim is one indexed UPDATE, so it costs nothing against the webhook deadline. |

## Route notes

What the hand route tables carried that the header migration (2026-10-27) did not move above a handler, by mount: each row whose handler already had a comment, whose description ran over 400 characters, or that matched no single handler, written as `**METHOD /full/path** (auth): description`, then the prose that sat under that mount's heading. Printed by `node scripts/migrate-headers.js route-notes` from this file at e243e5aa and hand prose from here on; the generated tables above are the route list. Nine rows matched no handler: seven put several routes or an elided path in one row, and two describe routes the walker no longer finds (`POST /api/shifts` and `GET /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list-data`); all nine are kept as they were.

### Authentication - `/api/auth`

- **POST /api/auth/register** (No): Create account, auto-create onboarding_progress row
- **POST /api/auth/register-pre-hired** (No): Create account with `pre_hired=true` - the application form still runs but on submit the user lands at `'hired'` instead of `'applied'`. Backs the open `hiring.drbartender.com/onboarding` URL.
- **POST /api/auth/claim-pre-hire** (Yes): Flag an already-logged-in user as `pre_hired=true`. Staff-only. If status is `'applied'`, also promotes to `'hired'` + seeds contractor_profiles + writes audit entries. Called by the `/onboarding` page when a returning recruit visits the URL.
- **POST /api/auth/login** (No): Validate credentials, return JWT (7-day expiry)
- **GET /api/auth/me** (Yes): Current user + `has_application` flag

### WebAuthn phone unlock - `/api/auth/webauthn` (`routes/webauthn.js`, mobile-admin spec 2026-08-13 s8)

Mounted INSIDE the `/api/auth/` prefix on purpose: the client's 401 interceptor excludes `/auth/` URLs from the session-expired dispatch, so a failed unlock can never log the phone out. All six routes ride `webauthnLimiter` (IP-keyed, separate from the login budget). `assert-verify` is the ONLY minting site for the 12-hour phone JWT (`{userId, tokenVersion, credentialId}`; the credential claim is informational, `middleware/auth.js` is unchanged); password login keeps its 7d mints. Challenges are server-issued, DB-stored, single-use (the DELETE is the claim), 5-minute TTL. Pre-signature assert failures share ONE public code (`WEBAUTHN_UNLOCK_FAILED`) so the unauthenticated surface cannot be used to enumerate credentials; the discriminating reason goes to Sentry only.

### Onboarding Progress - `/api/progress`

- **GET /api/progress** (Yes): Get user's onboarding progress, plus `documents_outstanding`
- **PUT /api/progress/step** (Yes): Mark a step as completed; returns the same shape as `GET /`

`:formKey` is allowlisted to `application` and `contractor_profile`. Every query is scoped to `req.user.id`, and the payload is capped at 64KB so a draft cannot be used as free storage under the global 1MB JSON limit.

### Outstanding onboarding documents

`server/utils/outstandingDocuments.js` is the single predicate behind two surfaces that must never disagree. It LEFT JOINs `applications` and `contractor_profiles` off `users`, deliberately: the sibling queries in `admin/hiring.js` INNER JOIN applications, which is right for funnel stats but would hide exactly the people this exists for, direct hires who never completed the application. Either storage location satisfies a document, hence the COALESCE.

- `outstandingFor(userId)` - the recruit's own to-do. **Both** documents, across the broad `ONBOARDING_STATUSES` (including `applied`/`interviewing`). Surfaced by `GET /api/progress` as `documents_outstanding`, rendered persistently in `client/src/components/Layout.js` so it follows them through every onboarding step.
- `listUncertifiedStaffable()` - the admin compliance alert. **Certification only**, and only across `STAFFABLE_STATUSES` (`submitted`/`reviewed`/`approved`), which mirrors the assignment gate at `server/routes/shifts.approval.js:233`. A LATERAL join attaches each worker's soonest upcoming shift so a booked worker outranks a merely-eligible one. Served by `GET /api/admin/hiring/uncertified` and rendered as one named row per person in the Needs-attention strip. The client applies the Staffing tab's 14-day horizon to that escalation (2026-08-17): a booking further out reverts to the standing "eligible" row rather than dropping, because a certification takes lead time to obtain and the person must never fall off the tab.

The two scopes differ on purpose. Sized against production, a single predicate covering both documents across all onboarding statuses returns 50 rows into a 6-row strip: every one of the 50 lacks a resume (a nice-to-have on people already hired) while only 2 lack a certification. A missing resume stays visible per-person on `DocumentsTab`; it is not an alert.

**Submit-gate contract (2026-07-26).** `POST /api/application` requires `resume` and `basset` **only when the submitter is not `pre_hired`.** A pre-hire may submit without them and finish later, because the notice above follows them through onboarding and `/contractor-profile` accepts the upload. A cold applicant still must supply both, because they have no way back in: the handler refuses a second submit, and `/contractor-profile` sits behind `RequireHired`, whose allow-list excludes `applied`. `isPreHired` is read from the `FOR UPDATE` row inside the transaction, never `req.user.pre_hired`.

Because a pre-hire's application row may now carry NULL document columns, `server/utils/contractorSeed.js` COALESCEs all six document columns against the existing profile row (`COALESCE(EXCLUDED.x, contractor_profiles.x)`) rather than assigning `EXCLUDED` outright. Without that, a pre-hire who uploaded at `/contractor-profile` before applying had both documents destroyed by their own fileless submit. The same change closes a pre-existing headshot wipe, which was always possible because a headshot was always optional.

### Contractor Agreement - `/api/agreement`

- **GET /api/agreement** (Yes): Get current agreement data (legacy compatibility)
- **GET /api/agreement/legal-text** (Yes): Fetch current version payload (clauses, acknowledgments, effective date) rendered by the client
- **POST /api/agreement** (Yes): Save signature, six v2 acknowledgments, SMS opt-in - then render PDF, upload to R2, email to signer
- **GET /api/agreement/download** (Yes): Short-lived signed R2 URL for the signer's most recent agreement PDF

#### Contractor Agreement Flow

The contractor agreement is versioned. The current version is defined in `server/data/contractorAgreement.js`
(`CURRENT_VERSION = 'contractor-agreement-v3'`; v2 stays in the `VERSIONS` map for prior signers). The React page at `/agreement` fetches the current version
payload from `GET /api/agreement/legal-text` and renders it dynamically (At-a-Glance bullets, 11 clauses, 6
per-clause acknowledgments).

On sign (`POST /api/agreement`), the server:

1. Writes all six `ack_*` booleans, signature data, IP, user agent, and `signature_document_version` to the
   `agreements` table inside a transaction with the onboarding progress update.
2. Post-commit: renders a PDF via `server/utils/agreementPdf.js` (pdfkit), uploads to R2 under
   `agreements/{user_id}/{version}-{timestamp}.pdf`, stores the key on the row.
3. Sends the PDF to the signer as a Resend email attachment. Email failures are logged to Sentry; the
   signature record is already committed.

`GET /api/agreement/download` returns a short-lived signed R2 URL for the current user's latest PDF.

V1 signers (pre-v2 schema) remain valid. The legacy `acknowledged_field_guide` and `agreed_non_solicitation`
columns are preserved for historical records; new v2 signers populate the `ack_*` columns instead.

### Contractor Profile - `/api/contractor`

- **GET /api/contractor** (Yes): Get contractor profile
- **POST /api/contractor** (Yes): Save profile + file uploads (alcohol cert, resume, headshot)

### Payment Info - `/api/payment`

- **POST /api/payment** (Yes): Save payment method, W-9 upload, marks onboarding complete. **Promotion gate (spec 2026-07-22):** the `onboarding_status -> 'approved'` flip runs through `promoteOnboardingIfEligible` (`server/utils/onboardingPromotion.js`). `hired` / `submitted` / `reviewed` always promote (admin-conferred or payment-import set); `in_progress` promotes ONLY when an `applications` row exists. Everything else is a no-op, including an already-`approved` re-submit.

### Application - `/api/application`

- **GET /api/application** (Yes): Get user's application
- **POST /api/application** (Yes): Submit application (file uploads, 21+ age validation)

### Admin - `/api/admin`

- **GET /api/admin/users/:id** (Admin): Full user detail (profile, agreement, payment, application, notes)
- **PUT /api/admin/users/:id/status** (Admin): Change onboarding status (hire, approve, reject, deactivate)
- **PUT /api/admin/users/:id/profile** (Admin): Edit contractor profile + payment info
- **PUT /api/admin/users/:id/permissions** (Admin): Update user role and permission flags
- **GET /api/admin/applications** (Admin): Paginated application list - derived `onboarding_progress`, `onboarding_blocker`, `flags` per row
- **GET /api/admin/applications/:userId** (Admin): Application detail + scorecard + unified activity timeline
- **POST /api/admin/applications/:userId/notes** (Admin): Add interview note (also writes to activity timeline)
- **PUT /api/admin/applications/:userId/interview** (Admin): Schedule/reschedule interview (optional confirmation email)
- **DELETE /api/admin/applications/:userId/interview** (Admin): Clear scheduled interview time
- **PUT /api/admin/applications/:userId/scorecard** (Admin): Upsert interview scorecard (5 dimensions, 1-5 each)
- **POST /api/admin/applications/:userId/move** (Admin): Stage transition (applied → interviewing → in_progress)
- **POST /api/admin/applications/:userId/reject** (Admin): Reject with reason (rate-limit: cannot re-reject already-rejected)
- **POST /api/admin/applications/:userId/restore** (Admin): Restore rejected applicant to Applied
- **POST /api/admin/applications/:userId/reminder** (Admin): Send paperwork-reminder email (24h cooldown per applicant)
- **GET /api/admin/hiring/summary** (Admin): KPI strip: new apps 7d / need-to-schedule / stalled / in-pipeline
- **GET /api/admin/hiring/search** (Admin): Cross-state applicant search (Applied/Interview/Onboarding/Active/Rejected/Unfinished)
- **GET /api/admin/hiring/uncertified** (Admin): Workers who can be assigned to a shift but have no alcohol certification on file
- **GET /api/admin/active-staff** (Staffing): Paginated list of onboarded staff. `?include_stubs=true` widens the status list to include `deactivated`. `onboarding_progress` is a LEFT JOIN and the `onboarding_completed` check is skipped for deactivated rows (2026-08-19, staff-hub spec): the imported placeholders were seeded with no progress row at all and a staffer who quit mid-onboarding has an incomplete one, so the old inner JOIN hid rows the hub's `deactivated_count` had already counted. The active set is byte-identical (approved/reviewed/submitted still require a completed progress row). Opt-in `?shift_id=<id>` (phone assignment sheet) adds `events_worked` and `home_distance_miles` (home to THAT shift's venue, in WHOLE miles, null when either side has no coordinates) to every row, through `server/utils/staffingMeta.js` (`candidateMeta`); raw coordinates never leave the server; 400 on a malformed id, 404 on an unknown shift. Without `shift_id` the row shape is frozen at its 20 legacy keys (pinned by `server/routes/shifts.staffingMeta.test.js`).
- **GET /api/admin/staff-hub/summary** (Admin/Manager): The ONE read behind the Staff hub chrome (spec 2026-08-19): roster counts (`active_count`, `deactivated_count`, `former_staff_count`, `imported_count`, sharing the roster feed's predicate family so count and list agree), admin decision counts (`new_applications`, `pending_reviews`), and `open_period`. The open pay run is DERIVED from the Chicago date by `server/utils/staffHubSummary.js` (`summarizeOpenPeriod`, pure) and LEFT JOINed to `pay_periods` on its UNIQUE `start_date`, because rows are minted lazily by `ensurePayPeriod` when the week's first shift accrues: a row lookup alone goes blank mid-week. Read-only by construction, it never INSERTs a `pay_periods` row (the `findOpenPeriodForDate` INSERT-fallback in `payrollLateTip.js` / `payrollClawback.js` is deliberately not copied). Managers get `null` for the three admin fields; a manager without `can_staff` gets `null` for the roster counts too (same gate as `/active-staff`). (`server/routes/admin/staffHub.js`)
- **POST /api/admin/managers** (Admin): Elevate staff to manager
- **DELETE /api/admin/managers/:id** (Admin): Demote manager to staff

### Drink Plans - `/api/drink-plans`

- **GET /api/drink-plans** (Admin): List all plans with filters
- **POST /api/drink-plans** (Admin): Create new plan (generates UUID token)
- **GET /api/drink-plans/by-proposal/:proposalId** (Admin): Fetch plan linked to a proposal. `?fields=day_of_contact` answers only `{ day_of_contact: { name, phone } | null }` (400 for an id that is not a positive integer, 404 when the proposal has no plan; both forms of this read take the plan with the lowest id when a proposal has more than one, so they name the same plan): the phone event detail reads that and nothing else, and it is the only form of this read the admin service worker stores (the full plan carries its write-capable token, internal notes and venue access notes). Any other `fields` value is a 400.
- **POST /api/drink-plans/for-proposal/:proposalId** (Admin): Get-or-create the drink plan for a proposal (used by `DrinkPlanCard`).
- **GET /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list-data** (Admin): Shaped data for the legacy client-side generation path (retired by the Potions mirror kill once `ShoppingListButton` moved to the regenerate endpoint)
- **GET /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list** (Admin): Fetch persisted shopping list + `shopping_list_status` + `finalized_at` (the modal opens read-only while the BEO is finalized)
- **PUT /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list** (Admin): Save shopping list edits (auto-saved by the modal while admin edits)
- **PATCH /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list/approve** (Admin): **Deprecated**, kept mounted for API compatibility: delegates to the `shopping_list_approve` comms action. Runs the action's idempotent side effects (atomic flip of `shopping_list_status` to `'approved'` + approved-snapshot write, then the derived BEO finalize) then sends the default unedited email on the default channels; the response carries `side_effects: { beo }`. New admin sends go through the compose-first `POST /api/comms/send`.
- **GET /api/drink-plans/:id** (Admin): Fetch single plan by ID (includes `has_consult_selections`, `shopping_list_source`, audit fields)
- **GET /api/drink-plans/:id/consult** (Admin): Fetch admin-side consult-form payload for re-populating the form, plus `recap` (readable lines with drink names, or null) for the shopping-list answers panel
- **PUT /api/drink-plans/:id/consult** (Admin): Save consult-form payload, regenerate shopping list as `pending_review` (via `drinkPlanConsult.js`). Hosted plans with no list: records the consult as source only, stages nothing, returns `list_staged: false`
- **PATCH /api/drink-plans/:id/shopping-list-source** (Admin): Flip active source between `planner` and `consult`, regenerate from chosen source (via `drinkPlanConsult.js`)
- **PATCH /api/drink-plans/:id/notes** (Admin): Update admin notes
- **PATCH /api/drink-plans/:id/status** (Admin (`drinkPlanWriteLimiter`)): Update plan status. A plain setter since 2026-09-22: no derived finalize, no `beo` in the response, and no UI caller (approving the shopping list is the review; kept for API callers and for resetting a plan to draft/pending). Zero rows on an existing plan is the finalize lock (409 `finalized`).
- **POST /api/drink-plans/:id/resend-nudge** (Admin): **Deprecated direct send**, kept mounted for API compatibility: delegates to the `drink_plan_nudge` comms action. Re-sends the Potion Planner invite (email + SMS) to the client; admin override sends even if the plan is already filled (skips archived events; SMS gated on opt-out via the action's channel availability). Links use the drink-plan token. New admin sends go through the compose-first `POST /api/comms/send` (the `DrinkPlanDetail` "Resend planner link" button opens the SendModal).
- **DELETE /api/drink-plans/:id** (Admin): Delete a plan
- **GET /api/drink-plans/t/:token** (Public): Fetch questionnaire by token (JOINs proposal for guest_count, num_bartenders, pricing_snapshot). The `pricing_snapshot` it sends is the public copy (`publicSnapshot.js`, spec 2026-10-16 section 4.3): no hidden adjustment, `breakdown`, `subtotal` or `total_price_override`, and `package.base_cost` folded to the package row the proposal page shows; the planner's `bar_rental` and `syrups.selections` are kept. Returns a locked payload `{ locked: true, proposalToken }` when the linked proposal is pre-deposit, so a stale emailed `/plan/:token` link renders a lock screen instead of the wizard (`isDrinkPlanPreBooking` allowlist, fails safe). Planner v2: payload adds `planner_version`, package slot config, and for v2 hosted plans a `hosted_coverage` per-drink two-tier block (`has_contents:false` = contents not entered, legacy hosted flow renders)
- **POST /api/drink-plans/t/:token/lab-cta** (Public): Enhancement Lab CTA click marker: write-once `lab_cta_clicked_at` (attach-rate funnel)
- **GET /api/drink-plans/t/:token/lab** (Public): Enhancement Lab shelves (`drinkPlans/lab.js`): submitted drinks with dossier enhancements + housemade-syrup pricing (server-computed via `calculateSyrupCost`, unpriceable ids never offered), addon price list (mocktail-gap pair + package-covered slugs excluded), proposal balance state, stored lab additions + their server-priced `lab_breakdown`. `state`: `not_ready` (pre-submit or planner v1) / `open` / `locked` (list approved or plan finalized; an open lab on a legal-hold booking also reads `locked`, so the page offers no addition, and the copy never names the hold)
- **PUT /api/drink-plans/t/:token/lab** (Public): Reconcile the client's lab additions (full desired state, idempotent; offered-surface allowlist + dossier syrup pairing + drift-drop). 409 outside the open window. A legal-hold booking is refused before any write with 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` ("This booking can't be changed online right now."), never the window's 409, which the page would turn into a locked bar calling the refused additions "locked in"; the extras fold's 409 `NOT_CHANGEABLE` stays behind it. Transactional: merges `labAdded` addOns + `labSyrupSelections` into selections (planner-owned addOns never touched), then FOLDS additions into the proposal - lab-owned `proposal_addons` rows + contract-safe reprice via `utils/proposalExtrasFold.js` (override moves by catalog delta; paid-in-full demoted when new money is owed, and restored once paid covers the total again) - and `refreshUnlockedInvoices` absorbs the new total into the open Balance/Full Payment invoice with each lab addon as its own line. A standing open `'Enhancement Lab'` invoice zeroes while a Balance absorbs (pre-fold migration + coexistence guard). Only when nothing absorbs and locked invoices exist (fully paid) does ONE open itemized `'Enhancement Lab'` invoice carry the UNINVOICED remainder (total − external − locked − other open contract invoices, so a standing unpaid Additional Services invoice is never double-billed; FOR UPDATE + guarded against a concurrent webhook pay+lock). Fails closed (400) when the proposal can't reprice. Response returns the folded `balance`. Post-commit: regenerates the shopping list (admin_set holds preserved), strips lab-covered syrup lines, stages `pending_review` (never touches an approved list)
- **PUT /api/drink-plans/t/:token** (Public): Save draft or submit selections (on submit: processes addOns into proposal_addons, recalculates pricing, sends admin email, auto-generates pending_review shopping list). Planner v2: allow-list adds `crowd`/`barPlacement`/`powerAtBar` (normalized) + the guestPreferences data-loss fix; hosted fence addons validate through coverageEngine and the mocktail-gap rule is re-derived server-side from RESOLVED picks (1 = pre-batched, 2+ = mocktail-bar), never trusted from the client; a v2 submit with `logistics.parking = 'paid'` server-derives the `parking-fee` add-on attach (per_staff, engine-priced, attach-only - removal is admin cancel-line-item); confirmation email appends the full-selections echo. An extras submit on a legal-hold booking answers 409 `NOT_CHANGEABLE` from the extras fold and saves nothing. A failed submit transaction goes to Sentry only when it is not an expected refusal (an `AppError` below 500), with the token's last six characters, never the token
- **GET /api/drink-plans/t/:token/shopping-list** (Public): Client read-only view, returns the list only once admin has approved it
