- **POST /api/drink-plans/t/:token/logo** (Public): Standard Menu logo upload (PNG/JPG, 5 MB cap, magic-bytes validation). Uploads to R2 and atomically merges `companyLogo` + `_logoFilename` into `selections` via the Postgres jsonb `||` operator
- **GET /api/drink-plans/t/:token/logo** (Public): Proxies the uploaded logo from R2 with `Content-Type` + `Cache-Control: public, max-age=86400`. Same-origin so `html2canvas` can capture it without CORS taint
- **POST /api/drink-plans/:id/logo** (Admin): Admin upload/replace for a specific plan's logo. Same validation + atomic JSONB merge as the token-gated route
- **DELETE /api/drink-plans/:id/logo** (Admin): Clears `selections.companyLogo` + `_logoFilename` via Postgres jsonb `-` operator. R2 file is not deleted
- **POST /api/drink-plans/:id/finalize** (Admin): Manual finalize. **Since 2026-09-11 finalize is derived, and since 2026-09-22 approving the shopping list IS the review:** a plan finalizes on its own the moment its list is `approved` (the derived predicate is exactly that; a hosted package owes no list, so hosted finalizes only from this manual route), never over unpaid extras. The shopping-list approve is the only derived trigger (it calls `autoFinalizeIfEligible`); there is no Mark reviewed step. The finalize UPDATE is the sole writer of `status='reviewed'` (stamped alongside `finalized_at`; the approve flip never touches status, so a refused finalize leaves the client's planner link working), so `finalized` still implies `reviewed` and a plan the client never submitted (an admin-built consult list on a `draft` with selections) finalizes on approve. `proposal_activity_log.details.trigger` records `manual` / `shopping_list_approved`. This route is the one click for a hosted plan (available from any unfinalized plan with selections), the unpaid-extras override (409 `unpaid_extras` unless `overrideUnpaidExtras: true`) and the way back after Unfinalize. Stamps `finalized_at`/`finalized_by` (selections non-empty, proposal not archived), schedules T-3 staff nudges for every approved staffer on every non-cancelled shift, writes `beo_finalized` activity log. Locks every mutation route on the plan until Unfinalize. Every refusal is a `ConflictError` with a code (`not_linked`, `archived`, `no_selections`, `already_finalized`, `list_not_approved`, `unpaid_extras`). `PATCH /:id/status` is a plain setter and finalizes nothing. Implemented in `server/utils/beoFinalize.js`, mounted into the drink-plans router.

The four shopping-list routes above (public `GET /t/:token/shopping-list` and the admin `GET`/`PUT`/`PATCH .../shopping-list[/approve]`) now live in `server/routes/drinkPlans/shoppingList.js`, extracted verbatim from `drinkPlans.js` (which was over the 700-line soft cap) and registered back onto the same router at their original positions.

The submit and lab files carry the same per-concern split (2026-07-22, behavior-inert moves; routes and exports unchanged): `submitSanitize.js` owns the selections allow-list + sanitizer and `submitNotify.js` owns the post-commit comms tail (confirmation emails, admin heads-up, lab follow-up scheduling), while `submit.js` keeps both money transactions; `labHelpers.js` owns the lab's shared state/pricing/sanitizer layer and `labListRefresh.js` the post-commit shopping-list rebuild, while `lab.js` keeps the GET shelves and the PUT money fold.

### Comms - `/api/comms`

- **POST /api/comms/preview** (Admin/Manager): Live-resolve the recipient + channels for an `{ action, entity_id }` pair and return the drafted (editable) message parts. Non-mutating: nothing is sent or written.
- **POST /api/comms/send** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): Run the action's idempotent side effects, then dispatch the admin's (possibly edited) message on the selected channels. Returns per-channel truth (`sent`/`failed`/`skipped` + skip reasons); each attempt writes a `message_log` row carrying `sent_by` + `body_edited`. Validates non-empty subject/body per selected channel and the 640-char SMS cap; an empty channel list is accepted only when the action genuinely has no channel to offer (side-effects-only confirm). An optional `silent: true` (with `channels: []`) applies the action's side effects and sends nothing, honored only for actions that declare `allowSilent` (currently `shopping_list_approve`, for the editor's quiet re-publish); it is validated before `ensureSideEffects`, so a rejected silent request never flips a side effect. The response also carries `side_effects_applied` and an allow-listed `side_effects` report (today only `shopping_list_approve`'s `beo: { finalized, reason?, unpaid_extras_cents? }`, meaning "the BEO is finalized now", so a Retry never clobbers it).

Compose-and-confirm client sends for the comms registry (`server/routes/comms.js`). The recipient is resolved server-side from the live client record; the request can never override the destination. Every admin-click client send routes through here (see Comms registry, below).

### BEO - `/api/beo`

- **GET /api/beo/:proposalId** (Auth + onboarded (admin/manager always; any onboarded staffer when the proposal has a non-cancelled shift)): Event-details payload - proposal/client/package, drink plan (without `token`; the consult rides as `consult_recap`, readable lines with drink names, with the raw `consult_selections` kept for one release while open staff tabs turn over), addons, shift_requests with `beo_acknowledged_at`, `shifts[]` (every non-cancelled shift with roles/`approved_by_role`/equipment/supply-run/cover flags + the viewer's own request), `menu_print` tri-state, viewer flags (`is_admin`, `is_assigned`, `is_acknowledged`), and `team_roster[]`. **Redaction:** `client.phone` and teammate phones are null unless the viewer is approved+active on the proposal (or admin/manager) - the anti-harvest gate that survives the read ungating. 404 (never 403) when the proposal is missing or has no staffable shift, so staff cannot enumerate proposal ids. Rate-limited per `req.user.id` via `beoReadLimiter` (60/15min).
- **POST /api/beo/:proposalId/acknowledge** (Auth): **Write, still assigned-only** (the read ungating did not extend to it; the UPDATE predicates are the enforcement). Staff: stamps `shift_requests.beo_acknowledged_at = NOW()` on every approved active shift_request the staffer holds on this proposal (gated on `drink_plans.finalized_at IS NOT NULL`; 409 if not finalized). A caller who is not on the event gets the assignment 409, checked before the finalize gate so the message names the real reason. Admin/manager: returns `{acknowledged:false}` (no-op).

Route paths and the `beo_*` identifiers are frozen; the 2026-07-22 staff event-details spec renamed only the words staff read ("BEO" is gone from the staff UI). Payload construction is shared with the shift-keyed surface below via `server/utils/eventDetailsPayload.js`. All three routes require `auth` + `requireOnboarded`.

### Staff event details (shift-keyed) - `/api/shifts`

- **GET /api/shifts/:shiftId/menu-print** (Auth + onboarded (admin/manager, or staff approved+active on the proposal)): Bar-menu print-file download. R2 proxy mirroring the logo route (signed URL, 8 s timeout), served `Content-Disposition: attachment`. The key must match `menu-print/<thisProposalId>/` and contain no traversal segment, so one proposal's file can never be served under another. 403 for a browsing staffer: downloading is an assigned-staff action. 404 when no file is posted. The guard and stream live in `server/utils/menuPrintFile.js`, shared with the admin `GET /api/proposals/:id/menu-print`.

Mounted from `server/routes/eventDetails.js`, BEFORE `staffShiftActions.js` (which applies `auth` router-wide, so falling through it would run `auth` twice). This is what the staff portal page calls; keying by shiftId removes the old three-feed proposalId hunt and makes deep links work cold. Both routes require `auth` + `requireOnboarded` and share the `beoReadLimiter` budget (60/15min per user).

### Cocktails - `/api/cocktails`

- **GET /api/cocktails** (No): List active cocktails with categories (explicit public columns; `recipe_review` never ships publicly, `ingredients` stays public by accepted spec decision)
- **POST /api/cocktails** (Admin): Create cocktail. `id` optional (server slugs from name, one `-2` retry); `is_active:false` creates an off-menu drink (needs-recipe Add-recipe path); structured recipe rows validated
- **POST /api/cocktails/:id/request-aliases** (Admin): Remember a client-typed custom request (`{alias}`) as this drink (shopping-list Match existing). Appends to `request_aliases` in a transaction; no-op on the drink's own name or an alias it already carries; 400 at the 20-alias cap or over 200 chars; 409 naming the drink when the text already equals the name or an alias of another drink IN THE MATCHER'S CANDIDATE POOL (recipe-carrying, on- or off-menu, either table); recipe-less rows such as an abandoned Add-recipe draft never block a match

### Mocktails - `/api/mocktails`

- **GET /api/mocktails** (No): List active mocktails with categories (same public-column trim as cocktails)
- **POST /api/mocktails** (Admin): Create mocktail (same optional-id slug + recipe validation as cocktails; mocktails now carry `ingredients` + `recipe_review`)
- **POST /api/mocktails/:id/request-aliases** (Admin): Same alias-append contract as cocktails (shared `appendRequestAlias` in `routes/potions.js`)

### Packages (planner v2 package model) - `/api/admin/packages`

- **GET /api/admin/packages** (Admin): Package ladder (retired included) + package_items counts
- **GET /api/admin/packages/:id** (Admin): Package + its structured contents rows
- **PUT /api/admin/packages/:id** (Admin): Slots config (`slot_count`, `slot_kind` hard/featured) + `is_active` only; pricing stays out of this surface
- **POST /api/admin/packages/:id/items** (Admin): Add a category-par row (`category`, `par_per_100`, `unit`, split-par `eligible_item_ids` verified against par_items)
- **PUT /api/admin/packages/:id/items/:itemId** (Admin): Update a contents row
- **DELETE /api/admin/packages/:id/items/:itemId** (Admin): Remove a contents row
- **GET /api/admin/packages/:id/makeability** (Admin): Every active drink classified against the package via coverageEngine: covered / fenced (+$/guest) / unmakeable / no_recipe
- **GET /api/admin/packages/:id/margin** (Admin): Directional margin sketch (`?guests&hours&labor&supplies`; knob defaults from app_settings margin_* keys); never bills anyone

### Potions (bar program) - `/api/potions`

- **GET /api/potions/pars** (Admin): Active par catalog; each row carries `used_by` `[ {id,name,table} ]` (drinks whose recipes resolve to it)
- **POST /api/potions/pars** (Admin): Create catalog item (server-generated slug id, `-2` retry on collision)
- **PUT /api/potions/pars/:id** (Admin): Partial update (item/size/qty_per_100/section/role/spirit_key/style_key/paired_spirits/ingredient_aliases/in_full_bar/sort_order/cost/recipe_qty_per_100). `cost` and `recipe_qty_per_100` clear with `null` or `''`; both per-100 quantities cap at 10000
- **DELETE /api/potions/pars/:id** (Admin): Soft delete (`is_active=false`); 409 while any recipe resolves to the row
- **POST /api/potions/pars/reorder** (Admin): Bulk sort_order (unnest pattern, 500 max)
- **GET /api/potions/preview** (Admin): Run the generator against the live catalog with a synthetic input (`?guests=1..1000&mode=full_bar|spirit_driven`); read-only

Regenerate (mounted at `/api/drink-plans` BEFORE the flat router, from `routes/drinkPlans/regenerate.js`):

### Proposals - `/api/proposals`

- **GET /api/proposals** (Admin): List proposals with filters. Base filters: `status` (single value or CSV, whitelisted), `search`, `source`, `view`. Money-board filters (mb-a lane): `from`/`to` (YYYY-MM-DD, validated server-side; malformed values ignored), `axis` (`event`|`sent`, selects the date column), `event_type`, `balance=open` (accepted rows where `total_price > amount_paid`), and `cohort` (`quoted`|`won`|`lost`) which mirrors the dashboard-stats `qSent`/`qAccepted`/`qLostValue` predicates exactly (and its own date column) and supersedes `status`/`view` when present. `X-Total-Count` carries the unpaginated total (the reconciliation surface for the Overview drill-outs). Default excludes paid + archived (those appear in Events / archive). Phone mode (lane ma-f1): `sort=created_at` joins the whitelist (the fallback order, addressable); `unviewed=1` is a server-side predicate, `last_viewed_at IS NULL AND status = 'sent'`; `envelope=1` answers `{ rows, total, bucket_total, unviewed_total, page, limit, has_more }` instead of the bare array (the array and the header are unchanged for every caller that does not send it). `total` follows the Unviewed predicate; `bucket_total` and `unviewed_total` are counted without it (the bucket's size and its never-opened count, whether or not the chip is on).
- **GET /api/proposals/financials** (Admin): Aggregated revenue + payments for the Overview money board (Band 2 - the former FinancialsDashboard). Accepts `?from=&to=&basis=` (booked|scheduled|paid) for date-range + lens filtering. `summary.leadSpend` reports Thumbtack acquisition cost (charged leads only, cents, attributed-to-proposal vs not, cash basis by lead created_at).
- **GET /api/proposals/:id/lead-cost** (Admin): Thumbtack acquisition cost for one proposal (the charged lead linked via `thumbtack_leads.proposal_id`); `{ leadCost: null }` when unlinked/uncharged. Drives the Acquisition line on the admin payment panel.
- **GET /api/proposals/dashboard-stats** (Admin): Server-side aggregations (booked / collected / outstanding / funnel / pipeline / monthly revenue series) for the Overview money board (Dashboard + Financials merged into one surface at `/dashboard`; `/financials` and `/financials?tab=payouts` now redirect there). Accepts `?from=&to=&basis=` for date-range + lens filtering; prior-period deltas included.
- **GET /api/proposals/metrics-split** (Admin/Manager): Funnel sent/accepted math split by `by=source|event_type` (required, whitelisted; anything else 400s) with optional `?from=&to=` (YYYY-MM-DD, half-open via the shared `dateClause`; malformed ignored). Native-era only (no `basis`/`include_cc` - the frozen CC ledger keeps no type/source detail, so this endpoint never reads the cc tables). Query-time vocabulary normalization merges the twin event-type vocabularies (native slug + Thumbtack human string) and folds NULL/empty to the `__untyped` sentinel; source folds NULL to `direct`. Returns `{ by, filters, segments: [{ key, sent:{count,value}, won:{count,value}, closeRatePct, pending }], truncated }` (keys only; labels resolve client-side). `sent`/`won`/`closeRatePct` mirror `qSent`/`qAccepted`/`qWinRate` per segment so the sum of segment `won.count` reconciles with the funnel `qAccepted` native leg. Segments capped at 12 (ordered by sent desc); the remainder rolls into one `__other` row and `truncated` reports it. Sibling of `dashboard-stats`; the two LAW endpoints stay byte-frozen.
- **POST /api/proposals** (Admin): Create proposal (auto-calculates pricing, creates client if needed). Rules are re-validated server-side via `validateProposalRules`. `send_now: true` creates the proposal as `sent`, creates its invoice in-transaction, and emails the client; otherwise it lands as a `draft`. `send_now` is fail-safe: anything but an explicit `true` is a draft. The `send_now: true` path stays for API compatibility, but the admin creation cockpit no longer uses it: it always creates a `draft`, then composes the initial send through `POST /api/comms/send` (action `proposal_send`: a row-locked draft→sent flip + invoice in one transaction, with drip enrollment post-commit). A past-2:00-AM duration is refused 400 (`fieldErrors.past_curfew`) unless the body carries `acknowledge_past_curfew: true`, which books it and audit-logs `proposal_booked_past_service_curfew`. Rate-limited per admin via `adminWriteLimiter` (10/min).
- **POST /api/proposals/calculate** (Admin): Preview pricing without saving. Optional `proposal_id` (int4; anything else ignored): prices the CONTRACT's hours, body hours minus that proposal's settled on-site extensions (contractDuration.js), for both the mandate basis and the snapshot, so the editor preview equals the save. The editor's request body is built in one place, `buildCalculateBody` in `client/src/pages/admin/proposalEditor/editorCore.js` (beside the bartender-override detection and the stored-gratuity read), the shared half of the desktop editor and the phone edit sheet (lane ma-e3).
- **GET /api/proposals/packages** (Admin): List service packages
- **GET /api/proposals/addons** (Admin): List add-ons
- **GET /api/proposals/:id** (Admin/Manager (`auth, requireAdminOrManager`)): Get single proposal with addons + activity log + `lead_call` (newest lead-call bridge outcome for TT-drafted proposals, null otherwise) + `consult_call` (2026-08-25, spec 5.3: the newest consult call chain across every consult on this proposal, carrying `status`, `answered_by`, `bridge_duration_sec`, `scheduled_at`, `detail` and `client_no_answer_at`; ordered `a.id DESC` so a reschedule's live chain wins over the abandoned one, and `null` when the proposal never had a consult, which is the common case). Also carries the refund-side derived money (spec 2026-09-15): `overpayment_cents` (the NETTED overpayment, same figure and key as the cancel-line preview), `off_contract_paid_cents` (the netting term, so the editor can net a hypothetical new total without a round trip), `max_refundable_cents` (largest single Stripe charge still refundable) and `max_overpayment_refundable_cents` (largest UNCREDITED headroom on one charge, i.e. how much of the overpayment can actually come back through Stripe). Also `settled_extension_hours` (always a number; row hours minus the contract's, negative after a hand-reverted row) and `contract_floor_hours` (MIN contracted hours over settled extensions, null without one) for the editor's add-on inversion and hint. Also `legal_hold` (spec 2026-10-16 section 5.3: `isLegalHold` on the loaded row's id, never the raw param; the editor and the phone edit sheet open read-only) and `adjustments_token` (`md5(COALESCE(adjustments, '[]'::jsonb)::text)`, the stale-lines token the editor sends back on save).
- **PATCH /api/proposals/:id** (Admin): Update event details, recalculate pricing, and re-sync the linked event shift (date/time/location/client) when the proposal has been converted. A draft→sent transition creates the proposal's invoice in the same DB transaction. Accepts an optional `change_request_id` in the body that links the edit to a pending `proposal_change_requests` row: the request is stamped `approved` in the same transaction as the edit, the standard admin edit email is suppressed (the client gets the change-request decision email instead), and the regular money + status reconciliation runs. **Notify-client contract (2026-07-22): the reschedule notice is opt-in** - the request names it via `notify: [{ type: 'event_details_changed', channels, email: {subject, body_text}, sms: {body} }]` (reviewed text composed at preflight; absent/empty list = nothing sends; a requested notice the save does not trigger is a 400 that rolls the edit back). Re-anchoring + balance-due recompute always run regardless. The staffing-gratuity disclosure stays automatic (billing disclosure) behind the suppression gate (sent by `server/utils/gratuityDisclosureNotify.js`, ledgered as `gratuity_disclosure` with the editing admin as `sent_by`), and the editor's booked-event reprice confirm (`proposalEditor/repriceSummary.js`) now names it: when money is paid, the gratuity was not set by hand, and the previewed gratuity total rises, the confirm says the client is emailed the new amount automatically, unless their email address is missing or has bounced (lane ma-e3a, 2026-10-05). When the save touches `event_date` / `event_start_time` / `event_duration_hours` and the result runs past the 2:00 AM curfew, it refuses 400 (`fieldErrors.past_curfew`) unless the body carries `acknowledge_past_curfew: true` (booked + audit-logged `proposal_booked_past_service_curfew`). Response carries `notifications: [{type, email, sms, email_error, sms_error, skip_reasons}]`. The client half of this contract is `client/src/components/comms/notifyDrafts.js` (the popup's drafts, the `notify` list, and one outcome toast per failure or real skip), shared by the desktop editor (`ProposalEditorForm.js` toasts the outcomes, its `NotifyConfirmModal.jsx` popup owns the drafts and the list) and the phone edit sheet's notify step; the save payload itself comes from `proposalEditor/patchBody.js`. Prices contract hours and stores worked hours (patchContractHours.js); refuses a duration CHANGE while an extension request is pending (`fieldErrors.event_duration_hours`). **Price lines (spec 2026-10-16 sections 3, 5.1 and 5.3; the guards live in `patchGuards.js`):** the route id must be plain digits inside int4 (400 `fieldErrors.id`), and that integer serves every query and the post-commit tail. A legal hold is refused 409 `LEGAL_HOLD` before any write, change-request approval included (early on the parsed id; binding on the locked row's id). A body whose `adjustments` differ from the stored lines (compared after the validator's coercions) must carry the current `adjustments_token`, else 409 `STALE_EDIT` (checked before the refund markers, so a stale tab always gets the 409 with Reload); a body that leaves the lines as stored needs none, and a client page view (which moves `updated_at`) never refuses a save. `total_price_override` is retired: a body may repeat the stored value in whole cents, clear it with null, or leave it out (carried forward); any other value, a half-cent spelling included, is a 400, and the save writes the stored value, never the body's spelling. Lines are validated by `validateAdjustments` (400 `fieldErrors.adjustments`); changed lines are written normalized, while a body that leaves them as stored leaves the column untouched (`COALESCE`), so their token holds, a jsonb number's scale included. A refund line may be kept, changed or dropped but its `refund_id` never minted or repeated (400). The `updated` activity entry carries `old_adjustments` and `new_adjustments` when the lines changed, and the response carries the new `adjustments_token`.
- **PATCH /api/proposals/:id/status** (Admin): Update proposal status. On a →sent transition, creates the invoice in-transaction (idempotent on `proposal_id`) and emails the client via `sendProposalSentEmail`. Rate-limited per admin via `adminWriteLimiter` (10/min).
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/resend** (Admin): **Deprecated**, kept mounted for API compatibility: delegates to the `proposal_resend` comms action. Re-sends the proposalSent email + initial-proposal SMS without changing status (validate-only RESENDABLE-status guard, no state side effect); dispatch owns its `message_log` writes. New admin sends go through the compose-first `POST /api/comms/send`. Rate-limited via `adminWriteLimiter`.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/portal-invite** (Admin): **Deprecated**, kept mounted for API compatibility: delegates to the `portal_invite` comms action. Emails the client their portal link (plain invite; the portal sits behind the OTP login, so no token rides in the email, and nothing is minted); no state side effect. Still logs `portal_invite_sent`. New admin sends go through the compose-first `POST /api/comms/send`. Rate-limited via `adminWriteLimiter`.
- **PATCH /api/proposals/:id/notes** (Admin): Update admin notes
- **DELETE /api/proposals/:id** (Admin): Delete a proposal
- **GET /api/proposals/t/:token** (Public): Fetch proposal by token (tracks views + geolocation). Payload built by the shared `buildPublicProposalPayload` (also the switch endpoint's response, so the two shapes cannot drift). Response includes `client_phone_prefill` (blank when the stored phone matches a known Thumbtack proxy, so the sign form never displays the proxy back to the customer) and `options_available` (whether the "See other packages" drawer entry link has anywhere to go: status sent/viewed, no `total_price_override`, not under legal hold (`isLegalHold` on the loaded row's id, as the switch refuses; the payload never says why), unsigned, not grouped-undecided, `amount_paid = 0`, >= 2 comparable catalog packages; the gate columns themselves never leak). Pricing leaves as `price_rows` (`[{ key, label, amount }]`, the page's service rows with hidden adjustments folded in on the server, `clientPriceLines.js`: into the package row, or, when larger than it, as one visible "Pricing adjustment" row, spec D3; the fold report never leaves) and a sanitized `pricing_snapshot` (`publicSnapshot.js`: no hidden adjustment, `breakdown`, `subtotal` or `total_price_override`, and a folded `package.base_cost`), never the stored snapshot (spec 2026-10-16 section 4.3; `publicLeak.test.js` asserts the raw body). A fallback to a "Pricing adjustment" row, a package-less snapshot, or a gap the snapshot's own arithmetic does not explain sends Sentry `public_rows_fold_fallback`, `public_rows_no_package` or `public_rows_unexplained_gap` through `reportPriceRowsFold`, by proposal id only, once per proposal per route per process. **An `archived` proposal is NOT served here: 404, same door-close as a voided invoice link**, so a stale emailed link cannot render a live page with working Sign & Pay. Note the asymmetry with `/t/:token/resolve` below, which is deliberately status-blind. Limiters: `requireUuidToken` runs first, then `publicTokenIpLimiter` (300 per 15 min, keyed by IP) as a ceiling on the client-supplied token key, then the token-keyed `proposalCheckoutLimiter` (60 per 15 min), which this route was moved onto off the shared IP-keyed `publicLimiter` on 2026-08-28 so that browsing can never spend the budget paying depends on.
- **POST /api/proposals/t/:token/sign** (Public): Client signature + acceptance. Body accepts an optional `client_phone`; a valid value overwrites `clients.phone` (replacing a Thumbtack proxy with the customer's real number) and resets `phone_status` to `'ok'`. Body requires `acknowledged_total` (400 without it, since 2026-08-28; the switch route has always required it): the UPDATE lands only if it still matches the row total, else 409 `TOTAL_CHANGED` (409 `ALREADY_ACCEPTED` when the row is no longer signable). Every 409 writes a `sign_failed` activity row (`code`, `acknowledged_total`, the proxy-validated `ip`, so a run of them reads as one client retrying or a leaked token being probed) and pages Sentry `proposal_sign_failed` (warning for `TOTAL_CHANGED`, info for a benign `ALREADY_ACCEPTED` replay), and the `signed` breadcrumb carries `acknowledged_total` too, so a blocked signature is never silent.
- **PATCH /api/proposals/:id/balance-due-date** (Admin): Override balance due date for a proposal
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/send-reminder** (Admin): **Deprecated**, kept mounted for API compatibility: delegates to the `payment_reminder` comms action. Emails (and can text) the client a balance-due reminder (balance = `total_price - amount_paid`, computed exactly as before, no money written; `ensureSideEffects` only enforces the NO_BALANCE_DUE guard). New admin sends go through the compose-first `POST /api/comms/send`.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/record-payment** (Admin): Record an outside payment (cash, Venmo, etc.) - triggers shift creation. Client receipt is **opt-in** via `notify_client: true` (absent/false = no receipt; suppression + `.invalid` placeholder gates apply even to a yes); the admin routine_finance notice always fires. Response carries `notifications` (`payment_receipt` entry when attempted).
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/create-shift** (Admin): Manually create event shift from a paid proposal
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/alternative** (Admin/Manager): Clone this proposal into a sibling "option" in the same group (creating the group if needed) for side-by-side compare. Pre-payment source only; 3-option cap. Returns `{ group_token, new_proposal_id }`.
- **DELETE /api/proposals/:id/group-membership** (Admin/Manager): Detach this option from its group; dissolves the group when one option remains. Refuses on a decided group.
- **GET /api/proposals/:id/group** (Admin/Manager): Group summary for the admin Alternatives panel: `{ grouped, group_token, decided, compare_visible_count, members[] }`. `compare_visible_count` counts the options the public compare page shows (`COMPARE_VISIBLE_STATUSES` in `utils/proposalGroups.js`, the same list `/group/:token` filters on); the panel offers "Copy compare link" only when the group is undecided, no option is a draft, and that count is at least 2, because otherwise the link opens a sign page or a 404 instead of a comparison.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/send-group** (Admin/Manager): **Deprecated**, kept mounted for API compatibility: delegates to the `proposal_send_group` comms action. Sends the whole comparison as ONE `proposalOptionsSent` email (one `/compare/:token` link), transitioning every draft option to `sent`. Defers per-option invoicing (to the winner's settle) and suppresses the per-option `proposalSent` email/SMS. Idempotent: a resend with no new draft options sends nothing. `PATCH /:id/status → sent` on a grouped option is blocked (409 `USE_GROUP_SEND`) so the solo path can't double-invoice/email it. New admin sends go through the compose-first `POST /api/comms/send`.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/archive** (Admin/Manager): Archive this proposal (scope `one`) or this plus every other open, unpaid proposal for the same client (scope `set` - covers formal comparisons and loose multi-proposal sets). Paid/converted targets 409 (`NOT_ARCHIVABLE`). Voids unpaid invoices in the same transaction; best-effort marketing/change-request reaps run post-commit. Note: on a client's FIRST settled payment (deposit/full/record-payment), the same sweep runs automatically via `sweepClientAlternatives` - their other open unpaid proposals archive as `option_not_chosen`; later balance/extras payments never sweep.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/stop-drip** (Admin/Manager): One-way "Stop follow-ups" (2026-09-22). Suppresses the in-flight `drip_touch_*` rows on this proposal and every open sibling with the same client + `event_date`, stamps `drip_stopped_at` on each, logs `drip_stopped` once on the target. Idempotent (`already_stopped: true` on a repeat). Operational touches untouched. A stopped proposal never re-enrolls on a later send transition (`scheduleDripForProposal` reads the stamp), never hands its drip off on archive and never inherits one (`dripSiblings.js`); only a new proposal starts a fresh sequence. Only open (sent/viewed/modified) siblings are stamped; a draft alternative sent later starts fresh. A client reply never stops the drip; this button is the manual off switch.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/cancel/preview** (Admin/Manager): Server-computed consequence preview for cancelling a BOOKED event (`server/routes/proposals/cancel.js`): `days_out`, `mode`, `refund_cents`, `refund_breakdown {gratuity_cents, excess_cents, fee_cents}` (all cents; math in `server/utils/cancellationMath.js`), affected `staff[]`, `comms_halted[]`, `email_preview`, and `blocking[]` (already_archived / completed / not_booked / autopay_in_progress).
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/cancel** (Admin/Manager): Execute the cancellation. Body `{ mode: 'client'|'drb', note, suppress_client_email, suppress_staff_notifications, confirm_last_name }`. Server-validates the typed last name (422 `LAST_NAME_MISMATCH`); requires a booked status (409 `ALREADY_ARCHIVED`/`ALREADY_COMPLETED`/`NOT_CANCELLABLE`); 409 `AUTOPAY_IN_PROGRESS` when a balance charge is mid-flight. One transaction: proposal → `archived` + `archive_reason` (`client_cancelled`/`we_cancelled`) + `cancelled_at`/`cancelled_by`/`cancellation_note`; linked shifts cancelled (shift_requests denied, BEO/shift comms suppressed); pending proposal-level `scheduled_messages` deleted; unpaid invoices voided; audit note + activity log. Post-commit tail: idempotent tip clawback (`clawbackTipsForCancelledProposal`, marker-coordinated with the `charge.refunded` webhook), marketing/change-request reaps, client cancellation email + staff shift-cancel notices (both suppressible; email over SMS).
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/cancel/refund** (Admin ONLY): Issue the agreement refund after a cancellation. Recomputes the target from `cancellationMath` (mode from `cancelled_by`, daysOut fixed at `cancelled_at`), caps at the refundable remainder, loops `server/utils/refundExecute.js` per refundable charge largest-first (deposit + balance) until met, attributing the gratuity portion via `proposal_refunds.gratuity_cents`. Idempotent (already-refunded caps the target; Stripe idempotency key per charge). Body `{ idempotency_key }`.  Honors the cancel dialog's `suppress_client_email` for the refund notice too (one checkbox, whole flow).
- **GET /api/proposals/:id/cancel-line/targets** (Admin/Manager): Enumerate cancellable pricing lines as typed targets (`addon:<slug>`, `bar`, `syrup:<id>`, `extra-bartender`, `adjustment:<idx>`, `gratuity`; `package` listed non-cancellable → cancel-event flow). An adjustment line marked `source` `'refund'` or `'agreed_price'` (spec 2026-10-16 section 6) is listed with `cancellable: false, reason: 'protected_line'`: removing it would raise the total and bill refunded money, or the agreed difference, again; the editor still changes it. A legal-hold booking answers `{ eligible: false, targets: [], reason: 'legal_hold' }`, so the admin breakdown draws no remove button. `server/routes/proposals/cancelLineItem.js` + `server/utils/lineItemCancel.js`.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/cancel-line/preview** (Admin ONLY): Server-computed removal preview: runs the SAME core as execute inside a rolled-back tx (old/new totals, overpayment cents, refund split incl. `manual_return_cents` for external money, locked-invoice notes, staffing warning, gratuity jar/notice flags, `fingerprint`). A protected adjustment line (a refund or an agreed price) answers 409 `PROTECTED_LINE` and writes nothing. A legal-hold booking answers 409 `LEGAL_HOLD` ("This booking is on legal hold and its lines cannot be removed.") right after the row lock, before any write.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/cancel-line** (Admin ONLY): Execute: fingerprint-guarded (409 `STALE_PREVIEW`), refuses a legal-hold booking (409 `LEGAL_HOLD`, right after the row lock, before any write) and a protected adjustment line (409 `PROTECTED_LINE`, the same server check as preview, before any write), commits the removal (fold-based contract-safe reprice, invoice refresh + delta-invoice reconcile, shift sync, lab-selection cleanup), THEN fires sequential per-charge refunds via `refundExecute` with `total_scope='overpayment'` (never re-lowers the folded total). Body `{ target, quantity?, new_rate?, reason, idempotency_key, notify_client, fingerprint }`. With `notify_client` and no refund owed, the client's removal notice renders only the core's `clientNotice`, decided from the locked row: a shown line (or any other kind) reads "We removed <label> from your event at your request.", and a hidden adjustment reads "We adjusted your package price by $X at your request." with X the change in the booking's total (the engine's zero clamp honored), never its label (spec 2026-10-16 section 4.3), while the response's `removed_label` (the admin toast) and the preview keep the real one.
- **GET /api/proposals/t/:token/resolve** (Public): Non-mutating: whether this proposal is one option in a comparison group + whether the group is decided, so `ProposalView` can redirect to `/compare/:token` without bumping `view_count` or flipping `sent->viewed` (which the full `/t/:token` GET does). **Deliberately status-blind, unlike `/t/:token`:** a GROUPED losing option is archived `option_not_chosen` with `chosen_proposal_id` set in the same transaction, and this is what redirects that client to the option they actually chose. Adding an archived filter here would strand exactly the clients the redirect exists to rescue. Note the scope: `sweepClientAlternatives` writes the same `option_not_chosen` reason on a client's other UNGROUPED quotes at first payment, and those correctly fall through to the not-found page. Pinned by `publicToken.archived.test.js`. Limiters: `requireUuidToken` runs first, then `publicTokenIpLimiter` (300 per 15 min, keyed by IP) as a ceiling on the client-supplied token key, then the token-keyed `proposalCheckoutLimiter` (60 per 15 min), which this route was moved onto off the shared IP-keyed `publicLimiter` on 2026-08-28 so that browsing can never spend the budget paying depends on.
- **GET /api/proposals/t/:token/payment-state** (Public): Non-mutating: the post-checkout settle poll's target. Returns `{ status, amount_paid, total_price, payment_type }` in DOLLARS and touches nothing, no `view_count` bump, no `last_viewed_at`, no `sent->viewed` flip, no activity row, so a thirteen-attempt poll cannot record thirteen views of a page the client is staring at once (the full `/t/:token` GET above does all four on every call). 404 on an unknown token and on an `archived` proposal, stated in its own SQL rather than inherited; `/t/:token/resolve` above stays deliberately status-blind. Limiters: `requireUuidToken` runs first, then `publicTokenIpLimiter` (300 per 15 min, keyed by IP), then the token-keyed `proposalPollLimiter` (40 per 15 min), its own bucket so a settle can never spend the checkout's budget.
- **GET /api/proposals/group/:token** (Public): Compare-page data for an option group: shared event header + per-option public-safe fields (`package_id`/name/slug/category/pricing_type/total/deposit + `floor_reason`/`billed_guests`/`floor_applied` extracted from the STORED pricing_snapshot). The compare matrix renders the stored totals and floor fields as-is, never a live reprice (live `POST /public/calculate` pricing belongs to the quote wizard; the proposal page's own comparison prices through `POST /t/:token/options` instead). On `publicReadLimiter` (100/15min; read-only, shares the client's IP budget with proposal loads). 404 while every option is an unsent draft. Positive-allowlist projection (no admin_notes/stripe/signature/PII).
- **GET /api/proposals/group/:token/preview** (Admin/Manager): Same as `/group/:token` but ignores the visibility gate (preview draft options before send), and each option carries `client_visible`. Read by the admin-host page `/compare/:token/preview` (`ProposalCompare` in preview mode: no redirects, choose buttons off, a banner naming the options the client cannot see), opened from the Alternatives panel's "Preview comparison".
- **POST /api/proposals/t/:token/options** (Public): Priced option set for the proposal's "see other options" panel (`proposals/publicOptions.js`). Body carries only a SELECTION (`tier_addon_id`, `extra_addon_ids`); every pricing input - guests, hours, bars, adjustments, gratuity floor - is read off the proposal row, never the request. Prices each active non-class package (plus the proposal's own, even if retired) through `priceProposedState` with a pre-fetched catalog, so the quote equals what a commit would compute; a rule violation is a per-option `available:false` + reason rather than a failed request. Returns `comparable:false` with no options for a `total_price_override` proposal (never re-price a negotiated number) or an already-signed one. Add-ons are gated by `visibleAddonsFor` per option, so a byob-only extra is never priced onto a hosted column. Read-only despite the verb, but on its OWN token-keyed `optionsQuoteLimiter` (120/15min) rather than the shared IP-keyed `publicReadLimiter` - browsing re-quotes on every extras/tier toggle, and exhausting the shared bucket would make the next page load report the proposal as expired and stop the Stripe payment form mounting.
- **POST /api/proposals/t/:token/switch** (Public): The drawer's "Switch to this package" (`proposals/publicSwitch.js`): the ONE pre-signature write behind the options panel. Body carries a configuration (`package_id`, `tier_addon_id`, `extra_addon_ids`) plus `acknowledged_total` (dollars, compared in integer cents); every pricing input is read off the FOR UPDATE row. Guards on the locked row: status whitelist `sent`/`viewed`, no `total_price_override`, unsigned, `amount_paid = 0`, not on legal hold (`isLegalHold` on the locked row's id, spec 2026-10-16 section 5.3, with the same `SWITCH_NOT_AVAILABLE` answer and copy), not grouped-undecided; pending Stripe intents are canceled server-side first and a `processing`/`succeeded` intent refuses with `PAYMENT_IN_FLIGHT`. On an engine total that differs from the acknowledged cents: 409 `TOTAL_CHANGED` carrying a fresh quote (built by the SAME `buildOptionsQuote` that serves `/options`), nothing written. On match, in one transaction on one pooled connection: proposal row (package/total/snapshot, `num_bartenders` synced to priced staffing; never tip_jar/gratuity fields/deposit_amount), addon rows replaced from engine output, payment-status reconcile, `refreshUnlockedInvoices`, and a `package_switched` activity-log row (old/new package + totals + dropped add-ons + IP). Responds with the same public payload as GET `/t/:token`. `requireUuidToken` runs first, then `publicTokenIpLimiter` (300 per 15 min, keyed by IP) as a ceiling on the client-supplied token key, then the token-keyed `switchLimiter` (20/15min). Pricing identity shared with the quote via `proposals/optionsPricingShared.js`.

### Bar menu print file (admin) - `/api/proposals`

- **GET /api/proposals/:id/menu-print** (Admin/Manager (`beoReadLimiter`, the staff route's per-user read budget)): Download the posted print file (the Download button in the fold of Event Detail's Bar menu card, `client/src/pages/admin/menuDesigner/MenuCardFold.js`; the browser saves it through `client/src/utils/downloadMenuPrint.js`, which the staff BarMenuCard shares and which turns a 403/404/502 into words, since a blob request's error body is unreadable to `api.js`). An id past int4 is a 404 here and on the three writes (`getProposalRow`), never a pg 500. Keyed by proposal, so an event with no shift row can still be read. Streams through `server/utils/menuPrintFile.js`, the same per-proposal key guard, R2 proxy and `private, no-cache` + ETag the staff route uses. 404 when no file is posted or the key fails the guard (R2 is never fetched); 502 when R2 fails.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/menu-print** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): Upload or replace the print file (multipart field `file`). Accepts PDF / PNG / JPEG **by magic bytes** (the declared filename and content-type are not trusted); the sniffed type also chooses the stored extension. Size capped by `MAX_FILE_SIZE` (`abortOnLimit`). Stored at `menu-print/<proposalId>/<uuid>.<ext>`; the previously stored object is orphaned (no delete in `storage.js`, mirroring the drink-plan logo). Clears `menu_not_required` so the tri-state cannot contradict itself. The order is the R2 upload, then `setMenuPrintKey(pool, ...)` (an autocommit UPDATE with a rowCount guard, 404 when the proposal is gone), then `reaccrueDuty`.
- **PATCH /api/proposals/:id/menu-print** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): Body `{ not_required: boolean }`. Setting it true while a file is posted returns 409 (remove the file first) - enforced inside the UPDATE's WHERE clause, not read-then-check, so concurrent admins cannot land the contradictory state.
- **DELETE /api/proposals/:id/menu-print** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): Clears `menu_print_key` (R2 object orphaned); status is reported from the `RETURNING` row.

Mounted from `server/routes/proposals/menuPrint.js`. Staff print the bar menu and bring it framed; this is where admin posts the file or declares that no printed menu is needed, and reads it back. The three writes return `{ menu_print: { status } }` with the derived tri-state. The key is set only through `setMenuPrintKey` in `server/utils/menuPrintFile.js` (here and in the menu art Approve), and the POST and DELETE fire its `reaccrueDuty` (the $5 `menu_print` duty line) after their write lands.

### Menu art drafts (`/api/proposals`, spec 2026-10-01)

- **GET /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft** (Admin/Manager (`menuDraftReadLimiter`, 120/min per user)): `readDraftPayload` (`server/utils/menuDraftRead.js`): the draft (or `null`); its pieces, each with `provider`, `model`, `drawn_count` and `image_rev` (an opaque 16-hex digest of the stored key, non-null exactly when `has_image` is, new with every drawing, for the client's layer cache), stuck ones (heartbeat older than `MENU_ART_STALE_MS`) reported failed, "interrupted"; the brief, with `planner_image_rev` (`plannerImageRev` in `menuDrinkSource.js`: 16 hex of sha256 over `planner:` plus the planner image key, null with no image; the key itself never leaves the server); the plan diff `{ added, dropped, brief_edited, image_changed, dismissed }`; the derived print state with `approved_by_name`; `cap: { used, limit, remaining, resets_at }`; `configured: { openai, gemini }`; and `plan: { id, menu_style, drink_count, drinks, bar_summary }` (null with no plan, and a draft is then read-only). `inputs.has_reference` is true only while `reference_source` is `upload` and a key is stored. Print state comes from `derivePrintState` (`server/utils/menuPrintState.js`).
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft/generate** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): Multipart: optional `reference` (`isValidUpload` plus `.jpg`, `.png` or `.webp` only) and `planner_image_role`, `reference_source`, `art_direction`, `drb_mark`, `image_model` (required). 422 `MENU_NO_DRINKS` with no plan or no drinks; 429 `MENU_ART_CAP_REACHED` or `MENU_ART_TEXT_CAP_REACHED`. `reference_source: 'planner'` with no client image on the plan is 400. The reference uploads first (`menu-art/<id>/ref-<uuid>.<ext>`); with no file and the source still `upload`, Start over keeps the stored `reference_key` (409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED` if another Generate changed it meanwhile). Then one transaction, `lockCalls` first: release the old run's reserved rows and every other reserved row of the proposal (re-roll and sync rows a restart stranded), BEFORE this run reserves; upsert the draft in place with a new `run_id` and `version + 1` (the `approved_*` stamps and `art_version` survive), reset `content` to the words step pending (so no drink keeps `added_by_sync`), clear `dismissed_plan_hash`, replace the pieces (the background plus the first 8 drinks in plan order, stamped with `image_model` as `provider` and `MENU_ART_IMAGE_MODEL` or `MENU_ART_GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL` as `model`), reserve the words call, then the images, COMMIT. Supersedes any run in progress. 202 `{ draft_id, version }`, then `runGeneration` on `setImmediate`.
- **PATCH /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft** (Admin/Manager (`menuDraftEditLimiter`, 30/min per user)): Body `{ version, content?, inputs? }`, checked by `validatePatch` (spec 7.1). 409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED` on a version mismatch. A content edit before the words land is a 400 field error (`validatePatch`); an inputs edit is allowed then. Server-written keys are refused (`inputs.reference_key`, `inputs.image_model`, `added_by_sync`, `palette`), as are a changed drink key set, more than 8 drinks with art, more than 12 visible, or more than 4 visible without art (a count already over may only go down), and a `drinks` or `also_at_bar` list longer than 200 before it is walked. Writes only the sections sent and bumps `version`; a save that changes nothing writes nothing and returns the current version. Never touches the pieces, `art_version` or the words status.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft/pieces/:pieceKey/reroll** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): Body `{ note?, image_model }`. The key must be `background` or a drink in the draft's content (404 otherwise) that has art (400 otherwise). Before anything is reserved: 409 `MENU_WORDS_PENDING` while the words are being written, 409 `MENU_WORDS_NOT_WRITTEN` when they failed or were interrupted (Start over is the remedy), and 422 `MENU_NO_DRINKS` with no plan (the read-only draft). 409 `MENU_ART_PIECE_BUSY` while the piece is working, or pending (its run is still active), and not stuck by the database clock. One transaction (`lockCalls`, the draft row, then the piece row) releases any still-reserved row of that piece (`releasePieceReserved`, the last attempt's unspent reservation, whatever its run), reserves one image (429 at the cap) and resets the piece: a new piece `run_id`, pending, the note, `image_model` and its model id (a drink with art and no piece row gets one). 202 `{ piece_key, status }`, then `runPieceReroll`. Only that piece's `provider` and `model` change.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft/sync** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): **Update draft** (`menuDraftSync.js`). Body `{ version, image_model }`. 409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED` on a version mismatch, 409 `MENU_WORDS_PENDING` while the words step is still being written, 409 `MENU_WORDS_NOT_WRITTEN` when it failed or was interrupted, 422 `MENU_NO_DRINKS` with no plan. Recomputes the plan diff with fresh catalog maps. Added client-typed drinks get one words call first: a short transaction (`lockCalls`, the draft row, the image-cap headroom for the added drinks that will get art (`requireImageHeadroom`, so a sync at the image cap answers 429 without spending a words call), `reserveText` under the draft's current `run_id`), then `runWordsForDrinks` (which authorizes the row) with no client held, so a 429 at the text cap, a 409 or a 502 `MENU_WORDS_FAILED` refuses the sync before anything is written. Then one transaction (`lockCalls`, the draft row, then pieces): the version and words checks again, the plan reloaded through the client, each dropped drink's still-reserved rows released and its piece deleted, the added drinks appended (catalog drinks from the catalog fields, typed ones from the words call; `has_art` while fewer than 8 visible drinks have it; `added_by_sync: true`), their art reserved under one new piece run and stamped with `image_model` and its model id, `plan_fingerprint` reset, `dismissed_plan_hash` cleared, `version + 1`, COMMIT. 202 with the new `version`, then one queued task draws the added art one piece at a time.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft/dismiss-plan-change** (Admin/Manager (`menuDraftEditLimiter`)): Body `{ version }` (compared with the stored version first). Stores the current plan hash (`fingerprintHash`, fresh catalog maps) in `dismissed_plan_hash` under the version check (409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED` on a mismatch). It never bumps `version`, so an approved draft stays approved; the response carries the unchanged version. 422 with no plan. The plan-changed banner stays hidden until the plan changes again.
- **GET /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft/planner-image** (Admin/Manager (`menuDraftReadLimiter`)): Sends the planner image of the event's plan (lowest plan id, its key read by `plannerImageKeyOf`, the rule Approve and the brief's rev share); the key must sit under `drink-plan-logos/<planId>-`. 404 when the plan has none. Takes the same `?w=`.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/menu-draft/approve** (Admin/Manager (`adminWriteLimiter`)): Multipart `file` plus `version`, `art_version`, `expected_print_key` (the print key the client confirmed over, the GET's `print.print_key`; empty means none) and, when the draft's `planner_image_role` is `logo`, `expected_planner_image_rev` (the GET's `brief.planner_image_rev` the client rendered with; empty means it rendered with no planner image; ignored for any other role). 404 with no proposal or no draft. 400 field errors, before any upload, for a missing or out-of-range field (the rev must be empty or 16 lowercase hex), a file that is not a JPEG by its magic bytes, one whose header `sharp` does not read as exactly 2400 x 3000, or one that fails a bounded full decode (`failOn: 'warning'`, sharp's strictest, so a cut file with an end marker appended is refused too; `limitInputPixels` at the print size). Then the R2 upload to `menu-print/<id>/<uuid>.jpg`, then one transaction: the draft row `FOR UPDATE`; 409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED` ("The draft changed. Approve again.") when `version` or `art_version` differs from the posted values; 409 `MENU_WORDS_PENDING` or `MENU_WORDS_NOT_WRITTEN` (`requireWords`) when the words are not in; 409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED` when any piece the layout uses (the background and every visible `has_art` drink) is not `done` with a stored key (zero illustrated drinks is allowed), or, for the logo role only (the only time the plan is read), when the plan's current planner image rev is not the posted one, so a logo replaced, removed or added since the render is never stamped as approved; the proposal row `FOR UPDATE` and 409 `MENU_PRINT_CHANGED` when `menu_print_key` is not `expected_print_key`; `setMenuPrintKey` on the client (which clears `menu_not_required`); the `approved_*` stamps (the posted versions, the new key, the planner image key the rev was checked against when the role is `logo`, else null, `approved_at`, `approved_by`); a `proposal_activity_log` row (`menu_approved`, actor `admin`, details `{ draft_id, version, art_version, print_key }`); COMMIT, the transaction's last statement. Then `reaccrueDuty`, then the response, so nothing after COMMIT can roll back or skip it. A rollback orphans the uploaded object (the menu-print precedent). 200 with `{ version, art_version, print }`, `print` shaped as the GET's.

Mounted from `server/routes/proposals/menuDrafts.js`, right after `menuPrint` (before `crud` and `getOne`); it mounts its siblings `menuDraftSync.js` (sync) and `menuDraftImages.js` (the three image GETs); those three and Approve's `menuDraftApprove.js` (below), four files, share `menuDraftShared.js`. Every route runs `auth`, `requireAdminOrManager` and `asyncHandler`, takes an integer `:id` (400 when it is not digits) and answers 404 when the proposal does not exist. Generate, re-roll and both sync transactions open through `inLockedTx`: `lockCalls` first, then the `menu_drafts` row (`FOR UPDATE`), then piece rows, and a `40P01` at any statement answers 409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED`. Any R2 upload runs before the transaction opens, and the job is queued only after COMMIT, with the client released. `:pieceKey` arrives URL-encoded and must match `^(background|(cocktail|mocktail|custom):[a-z0-9-]{1,100})$`. A posted draft `version` (PATCH, dismiss, sync) must be a positive integer an int4 holds (`parseVersion`, 400 otherwise) and is compared with the stored version in JS before any SQL sees it. Generate, re-roll and sync answer 503 `MENU_ART_NOT_CONFIGURED` without `OPENAI_API_KEY` (no Sentry page) and 503 `MENU_ART_GEMINI_NOT_CONFIGURED` for `image_model: 'gemini'` without `GEMINI_API_KEY`; the GET, PATCH, dismiss and the image GETs work with neither key, so an existing draft can still be viewed and edited. Every image route checks its key with `assertKeyUnder` (the prefix, and no `..` or `//`) before `sendR2Object` (`server/utils/r2Proxy.js`) reads it. Approve is its own file, `menuDraftApprove.js` (below).

**Approve** is mounted from `server/routes/proposals/menuDraftApprove.js`, right after `menuDrafts`. It reserves nothing, so it does not take `lockCalls`; its locks are the `menu_drafts` row, then the `proposals` row, and a `40P01` at any statement answers 409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED`. No client is held across the R2 upload, and duty reaccrual fires only after COMMIT and release, so the accrual reads the committed key.

**The job** (`server/utils/menuArtJob.js`, with its siblings `menuArtJobSteps.js` for the piece writes and stored-image reads, `menuArtJobWords.js` for both words calls, and `menuArtJobErrors.js` for descriptors, sentences, Sentry and the 429 wait) runs the words (always OpenAI, the words prompt and schema from `menuArtPrompts.js`), then the background, then the drinks (image prompts from `menuArtImagePrompts.js`; a background sent with a style reference says to borrow its palette, texture and mood only), asking for every image through `menuImageProvider.js` with each piece's stamped `provider` and `model`, so a background drawn by one model and drinks by the other is normal. The background's tone comes from `menuBackgroundTone.js`; a Gemini drink is keyed out by `menuKeyOut.js`. The plan shape comes from `menuDrinkSource.js` and `menuAlsoAtBar.js`, validation from `menuDraftValidation.js` (font pairing ids from `menuFontPairings.js`). `has_art` goes on exactly the drinks Generate reserved pieces for, and before each drink the job re-reads the draft's content, skipping a drink a PATCH turned off or hid (its reservation is released by the settle). A style reference is read once per run and only its prepared copy is kept: `menuImageProvider.prepareReferences` always re-encodes it (so no EXIF or XMP, such as GPS or an owner name, reaches a provider), applies its EXIF rotation, caps it at 1536 px and refuses it from its header when it would decode past r2Proxy's 200 MB budget (`decodedBytes`). The job finds its reservations by `run_id` alone, so an event deleted before the job starts (`proposal_id` is `ON DELETE SET NULL`) still has them released. The job holds no connection across a provider or R2 call (standalone `pool.query` only), refreshes the heartbeats of its active and pending pieces before each call, makes every write conditional on its `run_id`, retries only a 429 (`Retry-After` capped at 60 s, 20 s when none is sent, at most 2 times), and captures its own failures to Sentry with `{ area: 'menuArt', step }` and the sanitized descriptor; the routes' `queue` backstop sends the same descriptor, never the raw error. Its `finally` fails every piece of its run still pending or working ("not attempted: <cause>").

**The cap and reservations** (`server/utils/menuArtCap.js`). One rolling 24-hour image cap, `MENU_ART_DAILY_IMAGE_CAP` (default 60), covers both providers. The caps, the image timeout and the stale age parse through `server/utils/envInt.js` (`readEnvInt`, the shared positive-integer env parser: `0`, blank or nonsense falls back to the default with one warning, so a typo can never uncap a provider). Every image is reserved inside the request that asks for it (Generate, re-roll, sync), in a transaction whose first statement is `LOCK TABLE menu_art_calls IN EXCLUSIVE MODE` (`lockCalls`): advisory locks are a no-op on the Neon pooler, a transaction-scoped table lock is not, and one lock order everywhere means a deadlock (`40P01`) answers 409 `MENU_DRAFT_CHANGED`. Under the lock it counts kind `image` rows of the last 24 hours that are not `released` and either inserts one `reserved` row per image or refuses with 429. Before reserving, Generate releases every reserved row of the proposal (`releaseReserved` for its old run and `releaseProposalReserved`), and re-roll and sync release the reserved rows of the pieces they touch (`releasePieceReserved`), so a stuck or superseded attempt's unspent reservation stops counting. Words calls reserve kind `text` rows against their own ceiling, `MENU_ART_DAILY_TEXT_CAP` (default 100), and never count against the image cap. Before every provider call the job moves that call's reservation from `reserved` to `sent` in one UPDATE conditional on the run still being current (`authorizeCall`) and calls only when one row changed, so a superseded run spends nothing further. At the end of a run `settleRun` settles each reservation by its own status, never the piece's: `reserved` (never sent) becomes `released` and stops counting; `sent` (billed, then a later step failed) becomes `failed` and keeps counting. Only 429s, which are not billed, are retried, so one reservation stands for at most one billed image. `capUsage` feeds the GET's `cap`, where `resets_at` is when the oldest counted image ages out.

### Remote Staffing Fee (admin send surfaces) - `/api/proposals`

- **GET /api/proposals/:id/remote-staffing-check** (Admin/Manager): Venue distance from `HOME_BASE`, active-staff counts, and the server-derived `suggested_fee_cents`. Geocodes the venue ON DEMAND when `venue_lat` is NULL and a street address exists (throttled; street-less venues are never geocoded, so `venue_distance_miles` stays null and the popup is disabled). Returns `{venue_distance_miles, staff_within_40, staff_uncounted, suggested_fee_cents, prompted, accepted, should_prompt}`. `should_prompt` owns the whole rule server-side (coords present, >= 40 mi out, fewer than 3 active staff within 40 mi, not already answered, proposal not yet accepted) so the client renders a decision instead of making one - the suggestion bands live only in `server/utils/serviceArea.js`.
- **POST /api/proposals/:id/remote-fee-prompt-answered** (Admin/Manager): Stamps `proposals.remote_fee_prompted_at`. Every choice (add suggested, add custom, send without) stamps it, so a repeat send never re-asks a decision the admin already made.

Mounted from `server/routes/proposals/remoteStaffing.js` (carved out of `crud.js` at the size ratchet, mounted before the greedy `/:id` in `getOne.js`). Answers "is this venue far enough out, and thin enough on nearby staff, that the client should be charged a Remote Staffing Fee?" for the two admin send surfaces. Read-only plus a one-flag stamp; the fee itself lands as an ordinary `adjustments` surcharge through the existing editor PATCH, never a new money path.

### Client-Portal Change Requests (admin) - `/api/proposals`

- **GET /api/proposals/change-requests** (Admin/Manager): Admin queue of client change requests (default filter: pending). Powers `/change-requests` dashboard. Joins proposal + client for the card display.
- **GET /api/proposals/:id/change-requests** (Admin/Manager): Per-proposal change-request history (pending, approved, declined, cancelled) - drives the `ProposalChangeRequestCard` on Proposal Detail. Also returns `package_names` (`{id: name}` for every package_id appearing in a diff, inactive included) so the card renders package names, not raw ids.
- **POST /api/proposals/change-requests/:id/decline** (Admin/Manager): Decline a pending request with a required `decision_note`. Stamps `status='declined'`, `decided_by`, `decided_at`, `decision_note`. Fires the client decision email + SMS via `notifyDecision`.

### Stripe - `/api/stripe`

- **POST /api/stripe/create-intent/:token** (Public): Create Stripe PaymentIntent (deposit or full amount, with optional autopay). `server/routes/stripeCreateIntent.js`. **Writes no gratuity to the proposal** (election-at-payment 2026-08-03): the client's tip-jar election is computed in memory, charged, and stamped into the intent's `tip_jar`/`gratuity_rate` metadata for the webhook to apply on success. It does still stamp payment preferences (`payment_type`, `autopay_enrolled`, and a default `balance_due_date`) plus the `stripe_sessions` row. Intent identity is (amount, election metadata), so an election-bearing pending intent is never reused and a stale one is cancelled. Limiters: `requireUuidToken` runs first, then `publicTokenIpLimiter` (300 per 15 min, keyed by IP) as a ceiling on the client-supplied token key, then the token-keyed `proposalCheckoutLimiter` (60 per 15 min), which this route was moved onto off the shared IP-keyed `publicLimiter` on 2026-08-28 so that browsing can never spend the budget paying depends on.
- **POST /api/stripe/payment-link/:id** (Admin): Generate reusable Stripe Payment Link
- **POST /api/stripe/charge-balance/:id** (Admin): Manually trigger off-session autopay balance charge
- **POST /api/stripe/refund/:id** (Admin (`auth, adminOnly`)): Issue a partial refund - auto-targets the largest refundable charge; no cross-charge spanning. Body `total_scope`: `'contract'` (default; Approach A, the refund corrects the total) or `'overpayment'` (returns money held beyond the contract and moves neither `total_price` nor `total_price_override`). An overpayment refund is capped at the netted overpayment MINUS any pending overpayment refund, asserted under the proposals row lock in the same transaction that writes the pending row (409-free: 400 `REFUND_EXCEEDS_OVERPAYMENT`), and must land on a charge carrying enough UNCREDITED headroom (400 `OVERPAYMENT_NOT_ON_A_CHARGE`) so it cannot reverse invoice credits while the contract stands. Unknown scope is 400 `INVALID_SCOPE`. Client refund notice is **opt-in** via `notify_client: true` (suppression + `.invalid` placeholder gates apply; the archived-status gate deliberately does not); response carries `notifications`.
- **GET /api/stripe/refunds/:id** (Admin/Manager (`auth, requireAdminOrManager`)): Refund history for a proposal, including each row's `total_scope`
- **POST /api/stripe/webhook** (Stripe): Handle `payment_intent.succeeded`, `refund.created` (refund reconciliation, spec 2026-09-15: the Refund object is the payload, so it survives the account API version that stripped the refunds list off the Charge; scope comes from the named pending row, else Stripe's `duplicate` reason or the netted overpayment), `payment_intent.processing` (bank debit in flight, spec 2026-09-14: records `processing` on `stripe_sessions`, one activity row, client and admin emails), `payment_intent.payment_failed`, `checkout.session.completed`, `charge.refunded` (tip clawback only), dispute events, and `payout.paid`/`payout.failed` (read-side payout mirror, livemode-gated, catch-and-ack). Structure: `stripeWebhook.js` is signature-verify + dispatch only; per-event handler bodies live in `server/routes/stripeWebhookHandlers/`. `paymentIntentSucceeded` discriminates a service-extension payment by the `service_extensions.invoice_id` lookup: it skips the `amount_paid` roll-up (off-ledger label) and, post-commit and post-release, runs the settle tail (`settleExtension` then payroll then staff greenlight then `finalizeExtension`); a payment landing after the request already closed alerts admins for a refund instead of extending the event.
- **POST /api/stripe/create-intent-for-invoice/:token** (Public): Create a Stripe PaymentIntent for an open invoice (balance / Additional Services / Service Extension), used by the public token-gated InvoicePage (handler lives in `server/routes/stripe.js`). Extension invoices are gated server-side: 409 unless the linked `service_extensions` row is still `pending`, unexpired, and carries a recorded terms acceptance (`EXTENSION_TERMS_NOT_ACCEPTED`), so a client who routes around the terms page cannot pay with no acceptance artifact. Carries no gratuity metadata, so an invoice payment never writes the proposal's gratuity.

### Stripe Payouts - `/api/stripe-payouts`

- **GET /api/stripe-payouts** (Admin/Manager (`auth, requireAdminOrManager`)): DB-only summary (in-transit, fee MTD/YTD, unmatched count, last-synced) + pending (in-transit) lines + payouts list. Never calls Stripe.

Read-side mirror of Stripe payouts + balance-transaction lines (`server/routes/stripePayouts.js`). All money in integer cents. No existing table is written.

### Clients - `/api/clients`

- **GET /api/clients** (Admin/Manager): List clients
- **POST /api/clients** (Admin/Manager): Create client
- **GET /api/clients/:id** (Admin/Manager): Client detail with proposal history + `consult_calls` (2026-08-25, spec 5.3: the latest consult call chain per consult this client has booked, newest slot first, capped at 10; `DISTINCT ON (consult_id)` collapses inside the subquery so the cap counts consults rather than attempts, and each row carries `consult_id` as the list key plus `status`, `answered_by`, `bridge_duration_sec`, `scheduled_at`, `detail` and `client_no_answer_at`; empty array when they never booked one)
- **PUT /api/clients/:id** (Admin/Manager): Update client

### Venues - `/api/venues`

- **GET /api/venues/search** (Public): Venue-name autocomplete - proxies Google Places (New), rate-limited
- **GET /api/venues/details/:placeId** (Public): Place-details lookup - resolves a suggestion to a structured venue address

### Shifts - `/api/shifts`

- **GET /api/shifts** (Yes): List shifts (staff see open upcoming; admin see **all**, cancelled included, as a bare array; see the filtering contract below). Admin only: `?scope=upcoming|past&limit=60&offset=0&needs_staff=1` switches to the phone Events feed: paged by EVENT, envelope `{ scope, offset, limit, total_events, scope_events, needs_staff_events, has_more, next_offset, rows }` (total_events = events in the current filter, scope_events = events in the scope regardless of the chip, needs_staff_events = events with a flagged shift, 0 on past; the sidebar / phone tab badge (`unstaffed_events`) counts SHIFTS, the chip counts EVENTS, so the two numbers may differ by design), rows carry `event_key` and `needs_staff` (the unstaffed_events badge predicate: not finished, open, and `openSlotsSql` > 0, the staffing rule BY ROLE from `server/utils/positionsNeeded.js`; pinned by `shifts.adminScoped.test.js`) plus `venue_city` / `venue_state` off the proposal (the phone card's town line, 2026-09-24; scoped rows only, the legacy array stays pinned without them). Admin rows in BOTH modes carry `proposal_balance_due_date` (the proposal's `balance_due_date`, 2026-10-06), which the desktop Status column prints under an owed chip; it joined the pinned legacy column set on purpose.
- **GET /api/shifts/unstaffed-upcoming** (Staffing): Pre-filtered upcoming + still-needs-staffing list (powers AssignToEventModal - replaces the full /shifts dump). "Still needs staffing" is `openSlotsSql` > 0, by role, the same fragment the badge counts with
- **GET /api/shifts/my-requests** (Yes): Current user's shift request history
- **POST /api/shifts** (Staffing): Create shift
- **PUT /api/shifts/:id** (Staffing): Update shift
- **DELETE /api/shifts/:id** (Staffing): Delete shift
- **POST /api/shifts/:id/request** (Yes): Request assignment to a shift
- **DELETE /api/shifts/requests/:requestId** (Yes): Staff (and a manager without `can_staff`) withdraw their OWN pending request; that delete carries `status = 'pending'` itself, so an approval that commits between the handler's check and its delete is not deleted (409 `request_changed`). Staffing (admin, or a manager with `can_staff`) deletes any request in any status: the drawer's and the phone sheet's Remove. It releases an out-of-area lock, re-accrues payroll on a completed event, DELETES that person's pending `shift_reminder` and `staff_thank_you` for that shift (deleted, not suppressed, so assigning them again queues a fresh reminder), and suppresses their BEO nudges. Every removal on the staffing branch writes one `admin_audit_log` entry, `shift_request_removed` (actor, target, request, shift, proposal, the status and role it had), a staffing manager's removal of their own request included; it is written directly after the delete, so a failure in a later step cannot lose it, and its request id is the one the DELETE returned. A withdrawal on the owner's branch writes none. It notifies nobody. Pinned by `shifts.removeReminders.test.js` and `shifts.removeReaccrue.test.js`.
- **GET /api/shifts/:id/requests** (Staffing): Get all requests for a shift
- **PUT /api/shifts/requests/:requestId** (Staffing): Approve or deny a request (sends SMS on approve)
- **POST /api/shifts/:id/auto-assign** (Staffing): Run auto-assign algorithm (dry_run for preview, or execute to approve top candidates)
- **POST /api/shifts/:id/cancel-or-unassign** (Staffing): Cancel a shift or unassign one staffer; optionally notifies affected staff via SMS
- **GET /api/shifts/by-proposal/:proposalId** (Staffing): All shifts for a proposal (array, supports multi-shift events). 400 for an id that is not a positive integer. Each shift carries `approved_staff`, `requesters` (every request that is not denied, pending or approved, a dropped one carrying `dropped_at`; each with `request_id`, `status`, `position`, `requested_positions`, `replaced_by_request_id` (set on a cover claim, null otherwise), `events_worked` and the derived `home_distance_miles`, to the tenth of a mile), `approved_by_role`, the out-of-area context, and `finished` (past its END INSTANT in the event zone, `shiftFinishedSql`). Read by the desktop `EventDetailPage` and the phone `EventDetailPhone`.
- **GET /api/shifts/detail/:id** (Staffing): Single shift detail: `{ shift, requests }`. 400 for an id that is not a positive integer. `shift` carries `finished` and `proposal_status`; each request carries `events_worked` and the derived `home_distance_miles` (to the tenth of a mile). Read by the desktop `ShiftDrawer`, the phone `AssignmentSheet` (for display, and again by `useSheetWrites.js` before every write) and the `/events/shift/:id` deep-link redirect (`ShiftDetailRedirect` in `App.js`).
- **GET /api/shifts/user/:userId/events** (Yes): A staffer's assigned upcoming events (powers the staff "my events" redirect).
- **POST /api/shifts/:id/assign** (Staffing): Directly assign a `user_id` to a shift (admin/staffing path; inserts an approved shift_request + notifies).
- **PATCH /api/shifts/:id/out-of-area** (Staffing): Attach, raise, lower, or clear the Out-of-Area Bonus. Body `{ amount_cents: int|null }`, integer cents, `0 < x <= 25000` (the DB CHECK carries the same bound); null clears an unlocked bonus. Once the bonus is LOCKED (a staffer was approved while it was attached) the amount may only go UP: any reduce or clear is a 409. Attaching to an already-staffed shift auto-locks when there is exactly one approved non-dropped worker; with 2+ it stays unlocked and every shift payload carries `unlocked_warning`. A same-value save that cannot stamp a lock is a true no-op: 200 with the current payload, no UPDATE, no audit row, no re-derivation. Every real mutation (amount change, or a same-value save that stamps the lock) audit-logs (`shift_out_of_area_set`) and re-derives duty lines post-commit.
- **(Drop / Cover) /api/shifts various** (Yes): Drop / Cover shift marketplace - drop, request-cover, claim-cover, emergency-drop, withdraw. Lives in `server/routes/staffShiftActions.js`, mounted under `/api/shifts`; see the route file for exact paths.

**The staffing rule, by role (lane staffing-rule-by-role, 2026-09-30).** Every "needs staff" answer counts BY ROLE: a slot is filled only by someone approved (and not dropped) for that role, an approval in a role the roster never declared fills nothing, an approval with no role on file takes the first role with room, and a roster that declares no roles (empty, NULL or malformed `positions_needed`) is one slot. One rule, stated three times and pinned together: `openSlotsSql(alias)` in `server/utils/positionsNeeded.js` (the SQL the scoped feed's `needs_staff`, `GET /shifts/unstaffed-upcoming` and `badge-counts.unstaffed_events` all read, with the `IS JSON ARRAY` crash guard inside it), `roleFill` in `server/utils/staffingClassification.js` (the JS `confirmStaffingIfFullyStaffed` reads, so a last-minute client is told the event is staffed only once every role is filled), and its client twin `roleFill` / `rowRoleFill` in `client/src/components/adminos/shifts.js` (the phone list and staffing card, the desktop Unstaffed tab and count, the staffing column `StaffingCell`, and the overview queue). `server/utils/openSlotsSql.test.js` asserts the SQL equals the JS on every roster shape. `autoAssign.js` counts Bartender slots only, by role, and is outside it.

**Cancelled-shift filtering contract.** Cancelling an event (the P6 cancel flow, the archive reap, or `cancel-or-unassign` mode='cancel') soft-cancels the shift - `shifts.status = 'cancelled'`, never a hard DELETE, because payroll joins and shift history depend on the row surviving (`server/utils/shiftReap.js`). Hiding it is therefore the *reader's* job, and every server-side open-shift reader does it: the staff branch of `GET /` (`STAFF_OPEN_SHIFTS_SQL`), `GET /unstaffed-upcoming`, and `badge-counts.unstaffed_events` all filter `status = 'open'`.

The BARE admin branch of `GET /` (the call with no `scope`, i.e. what `EventsDashboard`, `OverviewPage` and `AdminDashboard` fetch) deliberately does **not** - the Events dashboard's Past / All tabs exist to show cancelled events as history. So any admin surface that reads that bare array and presents it as *upcoming* or *needs staffing* must filter client-side, via `isCancelledEvent` / `selectUpcoming` in `client/src/components/adminos/shifts.js` (the single definition; `shifts.test.js` guards it). Skipping that is what once put a cancelled event at the top of the Needs-attention Staffing tab while the sidebar badge, computed server-side, correctly excluded it.

**The SCOPED mode is the exception and filters server-side** (`adminScopedShiftsSql`, `server/routes/shifts.queries.js`). Its bucket law: every shift lands in exactly one scope. Upcoming = the end instant has not passed AND `shifts.status` is not `cancelled` AND the proposal is not `archived`; Past = the complement (finished, or cancelled, or archived, whatever the date), which is where the muted Cancelled card lives. Both COALESCE the two statuses, so a NULL-status shift still lands in a bucket instead of vanishing from the phone. The page is cut by EVENT (`event_key`, the proposal id, or the shift's own id when it is a manual shift), never by shift row, so a two-shift wedding cannot split across pages. The phone Events list therefore does no client-side scope filtering, and `isCancelledEvent` is used there only to mute a card, not to hide one.

**Shift visibility asks "has it finished", never "what day is it"** (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-14-shift-lifecycle-design.md`). A shift's END INSTANT is defined exactly once, in `server/utils/shiftEndInstant.js`, as a SQL fragment every consumer imports:

- `end_time` parseable → `(event_date + end_time::time) AT TIME ZONE tz`, rolled to the next day on an overnight wrap (an 8pm-1am booking really does end the following morning). The wrap is decided against `start_time` when it parses, and against `WRAP_CUTOFF_HOUR` when it does not - no shift legitimately *ends* in the small hours of its own event date without having started the evening before;
- only `start_time` parseable (`end_time` NULL or malformed) → `start_time + the proposal's event_duration_hours (or ASSUMED_HOURS) + OVERRUN_GRACE_HOURS`. The end is **assumed, not deferred to midnight**. Deferring it was the lane's own P1 and a regression against the code it replaced: a 10am brunch with no `end_time` stayed "not finished" until midnight, outranked tomorrow in `findNearestApprovedShift`, and a CANT text meant for tomorrow denied and re-opened the shift the staffer had already worked. The grace is what keeps a shift running long from vanishing mid-service;
- neither parseable → end of that calendar day in `tz`. This last rung is the **fail-safe-toward-VISIBLE** one: hiding a shift too early is the bug, so a malformed time falls back rather than raising;
- `tz` = the proposal's `event_timezone`, `America/Chicago` when the shift has no proposal.
